Sub Makro1()

Function GetHammaddeHarcamaListesi(BaglantiliMPSList, MPSNo)
	TableStr = "MPS_HMD_LIST__" & GetRndString()
	Doc.CreateTableObject TableStr & "=STACK", "TMPCOPY"
	Set ML = Doc.GetTableObject(TableStr)
	ML.AddField2 "KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE", "CHAR", 2, 0, "L", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "KAYNAKMPS", "CHAR", 10, 0, "R", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM", "CHAR", 6, 0, "L", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "EVRAKNO", "CHAR", 10, 0, "r", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "MAMULSTOKKODU", "CHAR", 24, 0, "L", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "MAMULSTOKKODU_STOK00_AD", "CHAR", 50, 0, "L", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "SF_PAKETSAYISI", "DOUBLE", 10, 0, "R", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "SF_PAKETICERIGI", "DOUBLE", 15, 6, "R", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR", "DOUBLE", 15, 6, "R", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "SF_SF_UNIT", "CHAR", 6, 0, "L", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "KAYNAK_MIKTAR", "DOUBLE", 15, 6, "R", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "MPS_TRNUM", "CHAR", 6, 0, "L", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "R_KAYNAKKODU", "CHAR", 24, 0, "L", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "R_KAYNAKKODU_STOK00_AD", "CHAR", 50, 0, "L", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "R_MIKTAR0", "DOUBLE", 12, 3, "R", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "R_TRANSFERMIK", "DOUBLE", 12, 3, "R", "", ""
	ML.AddReportFields
	
	BaglantiliMPSList.GroupBy("EVRAKNO")
	Set M10TM1 = Doc.Select1G("fsdff4fsg", "MMPS10TM", "EVRAKNO='" & MPSNo & "'", "", "EVRAKNO", -1)
	ToplamUretim = M10TM1.SF_URETILEN(1)
	ToplamMiktar = M10TM1.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(1)
	Oran = Doc.Div(ToplamUretim, ToplamMiktar)

	For i = 1 to BaglantiliMPSList.GetRowCount()
		Set M10T = Doc.Select1("fsfwssf4s", "MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO='" & BaglantiliMPSList.EVRAKNO(i) & "'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='H'", "", -1)
		For j = 1 to M10T.GetRowCount()
			Qstr = "KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE='UP'&KAYNAKMPS='" & M10T.EVRAKNO(j) & "'&KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM='" & M10T.TRNUM(j) & "'"
			Set MLx1 = Doc.Select2("wedwfdrtetfg", BaglantiliMPSList.GetAppName, QStr, "", -1)
			if MLx1.GetRowCount() = 0 Then

				satirNo = ML.AddRow()
				ML.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
				ML.KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE = BaglantiliMPSList.KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE(i)
				ML.KAYNAKMPS = BaglantiliMPSList.KAYNAKMPS(i)
				ML.KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM = BaglantiliMPSList.KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM(i)
				ML.EVRAKNO = BaglantiliMPSList.EVRAKNO(i)
				ML.MAMULSTOKKODU = BaglantiliMPSList.MAMULSTOKKODU(i)
				ML.MAMULSTOKKODU_STOK00_AD = BaglantiliMPSList.MAMULSTOKKODU_STOK00_AD(i)
				ML.SF_PAKETSAYISI = BaglantiliMPSList.SF_PAKETSAYISI(i)
				ML.SF_PAKETICERIGI = BaglantiliMPSList.SF_PAKETICERIGI(i)
				ML.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR = BaglantiliMPSList.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(i)
				ML.SF_SF_UNIT = BaglantiliMPSList.SF_SF_UNIT(i)
				ML.KAYNAK_MIKTAR = BaglantiliMPSList.KAYNAK_MIKTAR(i)
				ML.MPS_TRNUM = BaglantiliMPSList.MPS_TRNUM(i)
				ML.R_KAYNAKKODU = M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(j)
				ML.R_KAYNAKKODU_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", ML.R_KAYNAKKODU, "")
				ML.R_MIKTAR0 = M10T.R_MIKTAR0(j) * Doc.Div(BaglantiliMPSList.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(i), BaglantiliMPSList.KAYNAK_MIKTAR(i)) * Oran
				NetTransfer = M10T.R_HM_ODONEMNET(j) + M10T.R_HM_TRANSFER_EDILEN(j) - M10T.R_HM_IADE_EDILEN(j)
				ML.R_TRANSFERMIK = NetTransfer * Doc.Div(BaglantiliMPSList.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(i), BaglantiliMPSList.KAYNAK_MIKTAR(i))
			End if
		Next
	Next
	Set GetHammaddeHarcamaListesi = ML
	Doc.DeleteTableObject MLx1.GetAppName()
	Doc.DeleteTableObject M10T.GetAppName()
	Doc.DeleteTableObject M10TM1.GetAppName()
End Function
Function GetKaynakMPSList(MpsNo, MpsTRNUM)
	TableStr = "__MPS_BLST" & GetRndString()
	Doc.CreateTableObject TableStr & "=STACK", "TMPCOPY"
	Set ML = Doc.GetTableObject(TableStr)
	ML.AddField2 "KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE", "CHAR", 2, 0, "L", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "KAYNAKMPS", "CHAR", 10, 0, "R", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM", "CHAR", 6, 0, "L", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "EVRAKNO", "CHAR", 10, 0, "r", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "MAMULSTOKKODU", "CHAR", 24, 0, "L", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "MAMULSTOKKODU_STOK00_AD", "CHAR", 50, 0, "L", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "SF_PAKETSAYISI", "DOUBLE", 10, 0, "R", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "SF_PAKETICERIGI", "DOUBLE", 15, 6, "R", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR", "DOUBLE", 15, 6, "R", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "SF_SF_UNIT", "CHAR", 6, 0, "L", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "KAYNAK_MIKTAR", "DOUBLE", 15, 6, "R", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "ORAN", "DOUBLE", 15, 6, "R", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "MPS_TRNUM", "CHAR", 6, 0, "L", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "MUSTERISIPINO", "CHAR", 10, 0, "R", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "MUSTERISIPIARTNO", "CHAR", 16, 0, "R", "", ""

	ML.AddReportFields
	
	ML.Empty
	Set GetKaynakMPSList = ML
	
	QStr = "EVRAKNO='" & MpsNo & "'"
	if trim(MpsTRNUM) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & "&TRNUM='" & MpsTRNUM & "'"
	End if
	' Set M10TM = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10TM")
	Set M10TM = Doc.Select1("ee4f5454t", "MMPS10TM", QStr, "", -1)
	
	satir = M10TM.GetCurrentRow()
	For i = 1 to M10TM.GetRowCount()
		siraNo = ML.AddRow()
		ML.SetCurrentRow(siraNo)
		ML.KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE = M10TM.KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE(i)
		ML.KAYNAKMPS = M10TM.KAYNAKMPS(i)
		ML.KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM = M10TM.KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM(i)
		ML.EVRAKNO = M10TM.EVRAKNO(i)
		ML.MAMULSTOKKODU = M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i)
		ML.MAMULSTOKKODU_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", ML.MAMULSTOKKODU, "")
		ML.SF_PAKETSAYISI = M10TM.SF_PAKETSAYISI(i)
		ML.SF_PAKETICERIGI = M10TM.SF_PAKETICERIGI(i)
		ML.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR = M10TM.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(i)
		ML.KAYNAK_MIKTAR = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10E", "SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR", M10TM.EVRAKNO(i), "")
		ML.SF_SF_UNIT = M10TM.SF_SF_UNIT(i)
		ML.MUSTERISIPINO = M10TM.MUSTERISIPINO(i)
		ML.MUSTERISIPIARTNO = M10TM.MUSTERISIPIARTNO(i)
		Oran = Doc.Div(ML.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR, ML.KAYNAK_MIKTAR)
		ML.ORAN = Oran
	Next
	Call KaynaklariHesapla(ML)
	Set GetKaynakMPSList = ML

	Doc.DeleteTableObject M10TM.GetAppName
End Function
Sub KaynaklariHesapla(ML)
	siraNo = 1
	Do
		if ML.KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE(siraNo) = "UP" Then
			Set M10T = Doc.Select1("dr5t678ji", "MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO='" & ML.KAYNAKMPS(siraNo) & "'&TRNUM='" & ML.KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM(siraNo) & "'", "", -1)
			if M10T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
				Set M10TM1 = Doc.Select1("dee44x", "MMPS10TM", "EVRAKNO='" & M10T.EVRAKNO & "'", "", -1)
				'KaynakOran = Doc.Div(ML.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(siraNo), ML.KAYNAK_MIKTAR(siraNo))
				KaynakOran = ML.ORAN(siraNo)
				For j = 1 to M10TM1.GetRowCount()
					Set MLXX = Doc.Select2G("wef4fwfwdf", ML.GetAppName, "KAYNAKMPS='" & ML.KAYNAKMPS(siraNo) & "'", "", "KAYNAKMPS", -1)
					KaynakMPSToplamMiktar = MLXX.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(1)
					if ListedeYok(ML, M10TM1.KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE(j), M10TM1.KAYNAKMPS(j), M10TM1.KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM(j)) Then
						satirNo1 = ML.AddRow()
						
						ML.SetCurrentRow(satirNo1)
						ML.KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE = M10TM1.KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE(j)
						ML.KAYNAKMPS = M10TM1.KAYNAKMPS(j)
						ML.KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM = M10TM1.KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM(j)
						ML.EVRAKNO = M10TM1.EVRAKNO(j)
						ML.MAMULSTOKKODU = M10TM1.MAMULSTOKKODU(j)
						ML.MAMULSTOKKODU_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", ML.MAMULSTOKKODU, "")
						ML.SF_PAKETSAYISI = M10TM1.SF_PAKETSAYISI(j)
						ML.SF_PAKETICERIGI = M10TM1.SF_PAKETICERIGI(j) '* Oran
						ML.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR = ML.SF_PAKETSAYISI * ML.SF_PAKETICERIGI
						ML.SF_SF_UNIT = M10TM1.SF_SF_UNIT(j)
						ML.MUSTERISIPINO = M10TM1.MUSTERISIPINO(j)
						ML.MUSTERISIPIARTNO = M10TM1.MUSTERISIPIARTNO(j)
						ML.KAYNAK_MIKTAR = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10E", "SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR", M10TM1.EVRAKNO, "")
'if trim(ML.EVRAKNO) = "2570" Then msgbox KaynakOran
'						ML.ORAN = KaynakOran * Doc.Div(ML.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR, ML.KAYNAK_MIKTAR) * 
						ML.ORAN = KaynakOran * Doc.Div(ML.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR, ML.KAYNAK_MIKTAR) * Doc.Div(MLXX.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR, ML.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(siraNo))
					End if
				Next
			End if
		End if
		siraNo = siraNo + 1
		if siraNo > ML.GetRowCount Then Exit Do
		if siraNo = 150 Then Exit Do
	Loop
	if IsObject(M10T) Then Doc.DeleteTableObject M10T.GetAppName
	if IsObject(M10TM1) Then Doc.DeleteTableObject M10TM1.GetAppName
	if IsObject(MLXX) Then Doc.DeleteTableObject MLXX.GetAppName
End Sub
Function ListedeYok(ML, KaynakEvrakType, KaynakEvrakNo, KaynakTrNum)
	ListedeYok = True
	QStr = "KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE='" & KaynakEvrakType & "'&KAYNAKMPS='" & KaynakEvrakNo & "'&KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM='" & KaynakTrNum & "'"
	Set ML1 = Doc.Select2("f455ff33gfg", ML.GetAppName, QStr, "", -1)
	if ML1.GetRowCount() > 0 Then ListedeYok = False
	Doc.DeleteTableObject ML1.GetAppName
End Function
Function GetBaglantiliMPSList1(KaynakEvraktype, KaynakEvrakNo, KaynakTRNum)
	Set GetBaglantiliMPSList1 = GetBaglantiliMPSList("UP", "xyz11_", "000000")
	Set tbl = Doc.Select1("dee44XF4", "MMPS10TM", "EVRAKNO='__KXXX__'", "", -1)

	if KaynakEvraktype = "UP" Then
		QStr = "EVRAKNO='" & KaynakEvrakNo & "'"
		if trim(KaynakTRNum) <> "" Then
			QStr = QStr & "&TRNUM='" & KaynakTRNum & "'"
		End if
		Set tbl = Doc.Select1("dee44XF4", "MMPS10TM", QStr, "", -1)
	Elseif KaynakEvraktype = "SP" Then
		QStr = "EVRAKNO='" & KaynakEvrakNo & "'"
		if trim(KaynakTRNum) <> "" Then
			QStr = QStr & "&TRNUM='" & KaynakTRNum & "'"
		End if
		Set tbl = Doc.Select1("dee44XF4", "STOK40T", QStr, "", -1)
	End if
	For i = 1 to tbl.GetRowCount()
		Set BL1 = GetBaglantiliMPSList(KaynakEvraktype, tbl.EVRAKNO(i), tbl.TRNUM(i))
		GetBaglantiliMPSList1.AppendTable(BL1)
		Doc.DeleteTableObject BL1.GetAppName
	Next
	Doc.DeleteTableObject tbl.GetAppName
End Function
Function GetBaglantiliMPSList(KaynakEvraktype, KaynakEvrakNo, KaynakTRNum)
	TableStr = "MPS_BGL_LIST__" & GetRndString()
	Doc.CreateTableObject TableStr & "=STACK", "TMPCOPY"
	Set ML = Doc.GetTableObject(TableStr)
	ML.AddField2 "KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE", "CHAR", 2, 0, "L", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "KAYNAKMPS", "CHAR", 10, 0, "R", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM", "CHAR", 6, 0, "L", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "EVRAKNO", "CHAR", 10, 0, "r", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "MAMULSTOKKODU", "CHAR", 24, 0, "L", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "MAMULSTOKKODU_STOK00_AD", "CHAR", 50, 0, "L", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "SF_PAKETSAYISI", "DOUBLE", 10, 0, "R", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "SF_PAKETICERIGI", "DOUBLE", 15, 6, "R", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR", "DOUBLE", 15, 6, "R", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "SF_SF_UNIT", "CHAR", 6, 0, "L", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "KAYNAK_MIKTAR", "DOUBLE", 15, 6, "R", "", ""
	ML.AddField2 "MPS_TRNUM", "CHAR", 6, 0, "L", "", ""
	ML.AddReportFields
	
	Set GetBaglantiliMPSList = ML
	if KaynakEvraktype = "UP" Then
		QStr = "EVRAKNO='" & KaynakEvrakNo & "'&TRNUM='" & KaynakTRNum & "'"
	Else
		QStr = "KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE='" & KaynakEvraktype & "'&KAYNAKMPS='" & KaynakEvrakNo & "'&KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM='" & KaynakTRNum & "'"
	End if
	Set M10TM = Doc.Select1("dee44", "MMPS10TM", QStr, "", -1)
	if M10TM.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function
	
	siraNo = ML.AddRow()
	ML.SetCurrentRow(siraNo)
	ML.KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE = M10TM.KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE
	ML.KAYNAKMPS = M10TM.KAYNAKMPS
	ML.KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM = M10TM.KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM
	ML.EVRAKNO = M10TM.EVRAKNO
	ML.MPS_TRNUM = M10TM.TRNUM
	ML.MAMULSTOKKODU = M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU
	ML.MAMULSTOKKODU_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", ML.MAMULSTOKKODU, "")
	ML.SF_PAKETSAYISI = 1
	ML.SF_PAKETICERIGI = M10TM.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR
	ML.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR = M10TM.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR
	ML.SF_SF_UNIT = M10TM.SF_SF_UNIT
	ML.KAYNAK_MIKTAR = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10E", "SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR", M10TM.EVRAKNO, "")
	Oran = Doc.Div(ML.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR, ML.KAYNAK_MIKTAR)
	
	Do
		Set M10T = Doc.Select1("dr5t678ji", "MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO='" & ML.EVRAKNO(siraNo) & "'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='H'", "", -1)
		For j = 1 to M10T.GetRowCount()
			KaynakEvraktype = "UP"
			KaynakEvrakNo = ML.EVRAKNO(siraNo)
			KaynakTRNum = M10T.TRNUM(j)
			QStr = "KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE='" & KaynakEvraktype & "'&KAYNAKMPS='" & KaynakEvrakNo & "'&KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM='" & KaynakTRNum & "'"
			Set M10TM1 = Doc.Select1("dee44x", "MMPS10TM", QStr, "", -1)
			if M10TM1.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
				satirNo1 = ML.AddRow()
				ML.SetCurrentRow(satirNo1)
				ML.KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE = KaynakEvrakType
				ML.KAYNAKMPS = KaynakEvrakNo
				ML.KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM = KaynakTRNum
				ML.EVRAKNO = M10TM1.EVRAKNO(1)
				ML.MPS_TRNUM = M10TM1.TRNUM(1)
				ML.MAMULSTOKKODU = M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(j)
				ML.MAMULSTOKKODU_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", ML.MAMULSTOKKODU, "")
				ML.SF_PAKETSAYISI = 1
				ML.SF_PAKETICERIGI = M10T.R_MIKTART(j) * Oran
				ML.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR = M10T.R_MIKTART(j) * Oran
				ML.SF_SF_UNIT = M10TM1.SF_SF_UNIT
				ML.KAYNAK_MIKTAR = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10E", "SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR", M10TM1.EVRAKNO, "")
			End if
		Next
		siraNo = siraNo + 1
		if siraNo > ML.GetRowCount Then Exit Do
		Oran = Doc.Div(ML.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(siraNo), ML.KAYNAK_MIKTAR(siraNo))
		if siraNo = 250 Then Exit Do
	Loop
	Set GetBaglantiliMPSList = ML
	Doc.DeleteTableObject M10TM.GetAppName()
	Doc.DeleteTableObject M10T.GetAppName()
	Doc.DeleteTableObject M10TM1.GetAppName()
End Function
Sub SendTableToExcel(Tablo)
	Set pe2 = Doc.CreateSObject("ExcelOutput")
	PE2.SetOrientation "P"
	PE2.PageB_ReportHead_std1 Tablo.GetAppName
	
	Alanlar = Tablo.GetFields()
	ncol = 0
	For i=0 To uBound(Alanlar,1)
		AlanAdi = Alanlar(i,0)
		if Cikacak(AlanAdi) Then
			ncol = ncol+1
			if ncol > 256 Then Exit For
			AlanTuru = Alanlar(i,2)
			If AlanTuru = "INT16" or AlanTuru = "INT32" or AlanTuru = "DOUBLE" Then
				Align = "RIGHT"
			Else
				Align = "LEFT"
			End if
			pe2.AddColumnDef Alanlar(i,0), Alanlar(i,3), Alanlar(i,4), Align
			pe2.SetRowValue_String -1, AlanAdi
		End if
	Next
	PE2.LineOut
	
	For satirNo = 1 to Tablo.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Excel 'e gÃ¶nderiliyor : " & Tablo.GetAppName & " - " & i & " / " & Tablo.GetRowCount
		PE2.InitRowValues
		
		ExcelSatir = -1
		ncol = 0
		For i=0 To uBound(Alanlar,1)
			AlanAdi = Alanlar(i,0)
			if Cikacak(AlanAdi) Then
				ncol = ncol+1
				if ncol > 256 Then Exit For
				AlanTuru = Alanlar(i,2)
				If AlanTuru = "INT16" or AlanTuru = "INT32" or AlanTuru = "DOUBLE" Then
					Deger = Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo)
					pe2.SetRowValue_Double ExcelSatir, Deger
				ElseIf AlanTuru = "DATE" Then
					OutputStr=Doc.StrToNorDate(Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo))
					pe2.SetRowValue_String ExcelSatir,OutputStr
				Else
					pe2.SetRowValue_String ExcelSatir,Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo)
				End if
			End if
		Next
		PE2.LineOut
	Next
	pe2.Show
End Sub
Function Cikacak(AlanAdi)
	Cikacak = True
	if InStr(1, AlanAdi, "_STOK00_") > 0 Then
		Cikacak = False
	End if
	if InStr(1, AlanAdi, "_STOK00C_") > 0 Then
		Cikacak = False
	End if
	if InStr(1, AlanAdi, "_IMLT00_") > 0 Then
		Cikacak = False
	End if
	if InStr(1, AlanAdi, "_IMLT01_") > 0 Then
		Cikacak = False
	End if
	if InStr(1, AlanAdi, "_CARI00_") > 0 Then
		Cikacak = False
	End if
	if InStr(1, AlanAdi, "_MMPS10T_") > 0 Then
		Cikacak = False
	End if
	if InStr(1, AlanAdi, "_STOK40T_") > 0 Then
		Cikacak = False
	End if
	if InStr(1, AlanAdi, "_MMPS10E_") > 0 Then
		Cikacak = False
	End if
	if InStr(1, AlanAdi, "REPORT_") > 0 Then
		Cikacak = False
	End if
End Function
Function GetRndString()
	GetRndString = CStr(Doc.Round(Doc.CDbl((Timer + Rnd) * 10000000), 0)) 
End Function

Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Set gg = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")

	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb1 = Excel.Workbooks.Open("c:\barkod\barkod.xlsx", False, True)
	Set Sh1 = Wb1.Sheets(1)

	For i = 1 to 65000
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Aktarýlýyor : " & i
		StokKodu = trim(Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value)
		if StokKodu = "" Then Exit For
		if Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "KOD", Doc.LeftJustify(StokKodu, 24), "") = "" Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Geçersiz Stok Kodu : " & StokKodu, "", MB_OK
		Else
			Barkod = trim(Sh1.Cells(i,2).Value)

			Doc.Load_Voucher Barkod
			D7KIDSLB.SKOD = StokKodu
			D7KIDSLB.SF_MIKTAR = 1
			D7KIDSLB.SF_UNIT = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", Doc.LeftJustify(StokKodu, 24), "")
			D7KIDSLB.EANORDUPLICATE = "1"
			Doc.Save_Voucher
		End if
	Next

	Wb1.Close
	Excel.Quit
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : ER2011 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : BUTCE
Hangi Tablo için : REP17S
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Bütçe Modülü Data Aktarýmý
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Dim MTable
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "GK_3", "CHAR",10,0,"L","","RP17SEGK1"

	Dim MTableT1
	Dim MTableT2
	Set MTableT1=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET1=STACK","IO")
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "SELECTED","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "EVRAKNO","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "ACIKLAMA","CHAR",100,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "EXPORT_FILENAME","CHAR",100,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "SONUC","CHAR",20,0,"L","",""

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU2",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par

	MTable.GK_3 = "BUTCE"
End Sub
Sub RaporListesiniGetir()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set MTableT1 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET1")
	MTableT1.SetRowAID 1,1,1

	Set R17SE = Doc.Select1("dde34r55", "REP17SE", "GK_3='" & MTable.GK_3 & "'", "", -1)
	MTableT1.Empty()
	if R17SE.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	For i = 1 to R17SE.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MTableT1.AddRow()
		MTableT1.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
'		MTableT1.SELECTED = "E"
		MTableT1.EVRAKNO = R17SE.EVRAKNO(i)
		MTableT1.ACIKLAMA = R17SE.ACIKLAMA(i)
		MTableT1.EXPORT_FILENAME = GetDosyaAdi(R17SE.ACIKLAMA(i))
	Next
	MTableT1.SetCurrentRow(1)
	MTableT1.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub
Function GetDosyaAdi(RaporAciklamasi)
	GetDosyaAdi = ""
	Sh1 = InStr(1, RaporAciklamasi, "##")
	Sh2 = InStr(Sh1+2, RaporAciklamasi, "##")
	if Sh1 = 0 or Sh2 = 0 Then
		GetDosyaAdi = "Hata!!! Rapor Açýklamasýnda Dosya Adi ##c:\DizinAdi\DosyaAdi.xls## þeklinde yazýlmalý"
		Exit Function
	End if
	GetDosyaAdi = Mid(RaporAciklamasi, Sh1+2, Sh2-Sh1-2)
End Function
Sub ButceyeAktar()
	Set MTableT1 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET1")
	if MTableT1.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub

	Set MTableT1 = Doc.Select2("wedwef", "MAKROTABLET1", "SELECTED='E'&EXPORT_FILENAME='" & Space(Len(MTableT1.EXPORT_FILENAME(1))) & "'", "", -1)
	if MTableT1.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Çalýþtýrýlacak raporlarýn karþýsýnda geçerli excel dosya adlarý girilmiþ olmalýdýr", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Set MTableT1 = Doc.Select2("wedwef", "MAKROTABLET1", "SELECTED='E'&EXPORT_FILENAME>='Hata!!!'&EXPORT_FILENAME<='Hata!!!'", "", -1)
	if MTableT1.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Çalýþtýrýlacak raporlarýn karþýsýnda geçerli excel dosya adlarý girilmiþ olmalýdýr", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
	if DosyaAdiKontrol(fso, MTableT1) = -1 Then Exit Sub
	Set Wsh = CreateObject("WScript.Shell")

	Set MTableT1 = Doc.Select2("wedwef", "MAKROTABLET1", "SELECTED='E'", "", -1)
	if MTableT1.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Hiçbir rapor seçilmemiþ", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("REP17S", "")
	Set R17KT = Doc1.GetTableObject("REP17KT")

	Set MTableT1 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET1")
	For i = 1 to MTableT1.GetRowCount()
		if MTableT1.SELECTED(i) = "E" Then
			Doc1.Load_Voucher MTableT1.EVRAKNO(i)
			Doc1.RunCommand("CMD_SET_WINDOWLESS")
			Doc1.RunCommand("RUN_REPORT_RUN1")
			Doc1.RunCommand("RUN_REPORT_RUN2_EXCEL") 
			ExcelDosyaAdi = Doc1.GetDocParameterValue("SONYARATILANEXCELCIKTISI") 
			ret = fso.CopyFile(ExcelDosyaAdi, trim(MTableT1.EXPORT_FILENAME(i)), True)
			if ret = "" Then
				MTableT1.SONUC(i) = "OK"				
			End if
			if trim(ExcelDosyaAdi) <> "" Then
				ToVitalis = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataRoot & "WX\ToVitalis.exe"
				Wsh.Run ToVitalis & " " & Chr(34) & trim(ExcelDosyaAdi) & chr(34)
			End if
		End if
	Next
	MTableT1.Refresh
End Sub
Function DosyaAdiKontrol(fso, MTableT1)
	DosyaAdiKontrol = 0
	For i = 1 to MTableT1.GetRowCount()
		KlasorAdi = KlasorYoksaYarat(fso, MTableT1.EXPORT_FILENAME(i))
		if KlasorAdi = "" Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox trim(MTableT1.EVRAKNO(i)) & " kodlu rapor için dosya adý hatalý", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
			DosyaAdiKontrol = -1
			Exit Function
		End if
		if fso.GetAbsolutePathName(trim(MTableT1.EXPORT_FILENAME(i))) = "" Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox trim(MTableT1.EVRAKNO(i)) & " kodlu rapor için dosya adý hatalý", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
			DosyaAdiKontrol = -1
			Exit Function
		End if
	Next
End Function
Function KlasorYoksaYarat(fso, DosyaAdi)
	KlasorAdi = fso.GetParentFolderName(trim(DosyaAdi))
	if trim(KlasorAdi) = "" Then
		KlasorYoksaYarat = ""
		Exit Function
	End if
	if not fso.FolderExists(KlasorAdi) Then
		fso.CreateFolder KlasorAdi
	End if
	KlasorYoksaYarat = KlasorAdi	
End Function

	EDITTEXT D="EVRAKNO" CAPTION="Rapor Kodu"  RO=Y
	EDITTEXT D="ACIKLAMA" CAPTION="Açýklama"  RO=Y

	EDITTEXT D="SONUC" CAPTION="Aktarým Sonucu"  RO=Y
ENDGRID

ENDFORM

		Exit Sub

	if MTableT1.GetRowCount() > 0 Then

		Exit Sub
	End if

		Doc.d7MsgBox "Hiçbir rapor seçilmemiþ", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK

			if ret = "" Then
				MTableT1.SONUC(i) = "OK"				
			End if
		End if

End Sub

	Next
End Function

End Function

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : ER2011 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : ENTEGRE
Hangi Tablo için : MUHA10
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : MUHA10
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("MUHA10","")

	donemsonu = MUHA20E.TARIH
	donembasi = doc.AyinilkGunu(donemsonu) 

'    QStr = "SELECT * FROM STOK10A"
    QStr = "SELECT STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE, SUM(TL_TUTAR) MALIYET FROM STOK10A"
    QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN STOK00 ON STOK10A.KOD=STOK00.KOD"
    QStr = QStr & " WHERE STOK10A.TARIH>='" & donembasi & "'"
    QStr = QStr & " AND STOK10A.TARIH<='" & donemsonu & "'"
    QStr = QStr & " AND STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE='M2'"
    QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.KOD>='AA001'"
    QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.KOD<='RZZZZ'"
    QStr = QStr & " GROUP BY STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE"

    Set S10A2 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("vedrfhg", QStr) 

		For i = 1 to S10A2.GETRECCOUNT()

			Set hata = Doc1.CreateSObject("BATCHUPDATEMODE")

			if i > 1 then
				Set MH20E = Doc1.GetTableObject("MUHA20E")
				Set MH20T = Doc1.GetTableObject("MUHA20T")
				nret = Doc1.New_Voucher()

				MH20E.TARIH = donemsonu
			End if

			MUHA20T.Empty
			MUHA20T.AddRow

			satirNo = MUHA20T.GetRecCount()
			MUHA20T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

			if S10A2.EVRAKTYPE = "M2" then
				MUHA20T.KOD(satirNo) = "710 01 001"
				MUHA20T.ACIKLAMA(satirNo) = "Hammadde Sarfý"
				MUHA20T.BORCFIYAT(satirNo) = ABS(S10A2.MALIYET(i))

				satirNo = satirNo + 1
			MUHA20T.AddRow
			MUHA20T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
				MUHA20T.KOD(satirNo) = "150 01 001"
				MUHA20T.ACIKLAMA(satirNo) = "Hammadde Sarfý"
				MUHA20T.ALACAKFIYAT(satirNo) = ABS(S10A2.MALIYET(i))

				satirNo = satirNo + 1
			MUHA20T.AddRow
			MUHA20T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
				MUHA20T.KOD(satirNo) = "151 01 001"
				MUHA20T.ACIKLAMA(satirNo) = "Hammadde Sarfý"
				MUHA20T.BORCFIYAT(satirNo) = ABS(S10A2.MALIYET(i))

				satirNo = satirNo + 1
			MUHA20T.AddRow
			MUHA20T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
				MUHA20T.KOD(satirNo) = "711 01 001"
				MUHA20T.ACIKLAMA(satirNo) = "Hammadde Sarfý"
				MUHA20T.ALACAKFIYAT(satirNo) = ABS(S10A2.MALIYET(i))
				
				satirNo = satirNo + 1
			MUHA20T.AddRow
			MUHA20T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
				MUHA20T.KOD(satirNo) = "151 01 001"
				MUHA20T.ACIKLAMA(satirNo) = "Hammadde Sarfý"
				MUHA20T.ALACAKFIYAT(satirNo) = ABS(S10A2.MALIYET(i))

				satirNo = satirNo + 1
			MUHA20T.AddRow
			MUHA20T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
				MUHA20T.KOD(satirNo) = "152 01 001"
				MUHA20T.ACIKLAMA(satirNo) = "Hammadde Sarfý"
				MUHA20T.BORCFIYAT(satirNo) = ABS(S10A2.MALIYET(i))

			
			End if
			nret = Doc1.Save_Voucher()

			if nret <> 0 then
				hata = doc.d7MsgBox(MUHA20E.EVRAKNO & " no.lu evrak kaydedilemedi. Ýþlem kesilmiþtir","Freedom",MB_OK)
				Exit Sub
			end if
		Next
	nret = Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
	Set Doc1 = Nothing
End Sub                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               

Ekran Formu
Uygulamalar Ýçin Baþlangýç Ekran Formu Ayarlarý
Baþlangýç Ekran Formu Bu Uygulama için : 
Bu Uygulama için Baþlangýç Ekran Formu : 
Baþlangýçta Bu Makro Çalýþacak : 
Alt Ekranlar Ýçin Ekran Formu
Alt Ekran Form Kodu : 
Yeni Alt Ekra Form Kodu: 
Alt Ekran Açýlmadan Önce Çalýþtýrýlacak Makro : 


----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : ER2011 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : ENTEGRE1
Hangi Tablo için : MUHA10
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : MUHA10/150 HESAP ALACAK/710 HESAP BORÇ
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("MUHA10","")

	donemsonu = MUHA20E.TARIH
	donembasi = doc.AyinilkGunu(donemsonu) 

'    QStr = "SELECT * FROM STOK10A"
    QStr = "SELECT STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE, SUM(TL_TUTAR) MALIYET FROM STOK10A"
    QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN STOK00 ON STOK10A.KOD=STOK00.KOD"
    QStr = QStr & " WHERE STOK10A.TARIH>='" & donembasi & "'"
    QStr = QStr & " AND STOK10A.TARIH<='" & donemsonu & "'"
    QStr = QStr & " AND STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE='M2'"
    QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.KOD>='AA001'"
    QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.KOD<='RZZZZ'"
    QStr = QStr & " GROUP BY STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE"

    Set S10A2 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("vedrfhg", QStr) 

		For i = 1 to S10A2.GETRECCOUNT()

			Set hata = Doc1.CreateSObject("BATCHUPDATEMODE")

			if i > 1 then
				Set MH20E = Doc1.GetTableObject("MUHA20E")
				Set MH20T = Doc1.GetTableObject("MUHA20T")
				nret = Doc1.New_Voucher()

				MH20E.TARIH = donemsonu
			End if

			MUHA20T.Empty
			MUHA20T.AddRow

			satirNo = MUHA20T.GetRecCount()
			MUHA20T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

			if S10A2.EVRAKTYPE = "M2" then
				MUHA20T.KOD(satirNo) = "710 01 001"
				MUHA20T.ACIKLAMA(satirNo) = "Hammadde Sarfý"
				MUHA20T.BORCFIYAT(satirNo) = ABS(S10A2.MALIYET(i))

				satirNo = satirNo + 1
			MUHA20T.AddRow
			MUHA20T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
				MUHA20T.KOD(satirNo) = "150 01 001"
				MUHA20T.ACIKLAMA(satirNo) = "Hammadde Sarfý"
				MUHA20T.ALACAKFIYAT(satirNo) = ABS(S10A2.MALIYET(i))

							
			End if
			nret = Doc1.Save_Voucher()

			if nret <> 0 then
				hata = doc.d7MsgBox(MUHA20E.EVRAKNO & " no.lu evrak kaydedilemedi. Ýþlem kesilmiþtir","Freedom",MB_OK)
				Exit Sub
			end if
		Next
	nret = Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
	Set Doc1 = Nothing
End Sub

Sub Makro1()

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("MUHA10","")

	donemsonu = MUHA20E.TARIH
	donembasi = doc.AyinilkGunu(donemsonu) 

'    QStr = "SELECT * FROM STOK10A"
    QStr = "SELECT STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE, SUM(TL_TUTAR)+SUM(TL_NONMIKTARTUTAR) MALIYET FROM STOK10A"
    QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN STOK00 ON STOK10A.KOD=STOK00.KOD"
    QStr = QStr & " WHERE STOK10A.TARIH>='" & donembasi & "'"
    QStr = QStr & " AND STOK10A.TARIH<='" & donemsonu & "'"
    QStr = QStr & " AND STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE='MG'"
   ' QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.KOD>='AA001'"
   ' QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.KOD<='RZZZZ'"
    QStr = QStr & " GROUP BY STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE"

    Set S10A2 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("vedrfhg", QStr) 

		For i = 1 to S10A2.GETRECCOUNT()

			Set hata = Doc1.CreateSObject("BATCHUPDATEMODE")

			if i > 1 then
				Set MH20E = Doc1.GetTableObject("MUHA20E")
				Set MH20T = Doc1.GetTableObject("MUHA20T")
				nret = Doc1.New_Voucher()

				MH20E.TARIH = donemsonu
			End if

			MUHA20T.Empty
			MUHA20T.AddRow

			satirNo = MUHA20T.GetRecCount()
			MUHA20T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

			if S10A2.EVRAKTYPE = "MG" then
				MUHA20T.KOD(satirNo) = "152 01 001"
				MUHA20T.ACIKLAMA(satirNo) = "Mamül Üretim"
				MUHA20T.BORCFIYAT(satirNo) = ABS(S10A2.MALIYET(i))

				satirNo = satirNo + 1
			MUHA20T.AddRow
			MUHA20T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
				MUHA20T.KOD(satirNo) = "151 01 001"
				MUHA20T.ACIKLAMA(satirNo) = "Mamül Üretim"
				MUHA20T.ALACAKFIYAT(satirNo) = ABS(S10A2.MALIYET(i))

							
			End if
			nret = Doc1.Save_Voucher()

			if nret <> 0 then
				hata = doc.d7MsgBox(MUHA20E.EVRAKNO & " no.lu evrak kaydedilemedi. Ýþlem kesilmiþtir","Freedom",MB_OK)
				Exit Sub
			end if
		Next
	nret = Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
	Set Doc1 = Nothing
End Sub

Function GetEtiketTable(EtiketFormu)
	if Doc.GetDocType = "STOK20" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK20_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK21" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK21_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK68" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK68_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "SAYM01" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = SAYM01_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK60" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK60_EtiketYarat()
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK25" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK25_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "TRACK25" or Doc.GetDocType = "TRACK251" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = TRACK25_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK29" or Doc.GetDocType = "STOK29I" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK29_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK36" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK36_EtiketYarat()
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK19" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK19_EtiketYarat()
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK26" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK26_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK63" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = Transfer_EtiketYarat(STOK63T)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK61" Then
 		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK61_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK24" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK24_EtiketYarat()
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "MMPS10" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = MMPS10_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "D7KIDSLB" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = D7KIDSLB_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "STOK46" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = STOK46_EtiketYarat()
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "SFDC32" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = SFDC32_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu, Miktar)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType0 = "TRACKIZ" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = TRACKIZ_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "OSF312" Then
		Set GetEtiketTable = OSF312_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	End if
End Function
Function D7KIDSLB_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Set D7KIDSLB_EtiketYarat = Doc.GetTableObject("D7KIDSLB")
End Function
Function STOK20_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")
	Set d7LB = Doc1.GetTableObject("D7KIDSLB")

	Doc1.CreateTableObject "xxx11dw=D7KIDSLB", "TMPCOPY"
	Set d7LB1 = Doc1.GetTableObject("xxx11dw")

	Set S20T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20T")
	Set S20E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20E")

	Set STOK20_EtiketYarat = d7LB1

	S20T.SafePack()
	if trim(S20T.KOD) = "" Then Exit Function

	For i = 1 to S20T.GetRowCount()

'		BarkodNo = Doc.Sayac0danAl("SERINO")
		BarkodNo = S20T.SERINO(i)
		Set d = Doc.Select1("de456789000", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Doc.LeftJustify(trim(BarkodNo), 30) & "'", "", -1)
		if d.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
'		if trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("D7KIDSLB", "KOD", Doc.LeftJustify(trim(BarkodNo), 24), "")) = "" Then
			Doc1.Load_Voucher BarkodNo

			d7LB.SKOD = S20T.KOD(i)
			d7LB.LOTNUMBER = S20T.LOTNUMBER(i)
			d7LB.SERINO = S20T.SERINO(i)
			d7LB.SF_MIKTAR = S20T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			d7LB.SF_UNIT = S20T.SF_SF_UNIT(i)
			d7LB.MPSNO = S20T.MPSNO(i)
			d7LB.MPSTM_TRNUM = S20T.MPSTM_TRNUM(i)
			d7LB.FOKOD = S20T.FOKOD(i)
			d7LB.AMBCODE1 = S20E.AMBCODE
			d7LB.AMBCODE2 = S20E.IMALATAMBCODE
			d7LB.AMBCODE3 = S20E.YANMAMULAMBCODE
			d7LB.REFTARIH1 = S20E.TARIH
			d7LB.CH_MUSTERI = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10TM", "MUSTERIKODU", S20T.MPSNO(i) & S20T.MPSTM_TRNUM(i), "EVRAKNO+TRNUM")
			if trim(d7LB.CH_MUSTERI) = "8Y001" or trim(d7LB.CH_MUSTERI) = "8Y002" Then
				Surtel = "S"
				SipArtNo = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10TM", "MUSTERISIPIARTNO", S20T.MPSNO(i) & S20T.MPSTM_TRNUM(i), "EVRAKNO+TRNUM")
				AmbalajTipi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40T", "PMIKTAR_UNIT", SipArtNo, "ARTNO")
				itemkod = Doc.GetCHStokKodu(S20T.KOD(i), d7LB.CH_MUSTERI)
				if trim(AmbalajTipi) = "MAKARA" Then
					itemkod = trim(itemkod) & "D"
				Elseif trim(AmbalajTipi) = "KANGAL" Then
					itemkod = trim(itemkod) & "C"
				End if
				DrumNo = Right("0000" & Doc.Sayac0danAl("YADU" & Mid(Replace(S20E.TARIH, "/", ""), 3)), 4)
				UretimTarihi2 = right(S20E.TARIH,2)+Mid(S20E.TARIH,6,2)+Mid(S20E.TARIH,3,2)
				YaduBarkod = "88" & UretimTarihi2 & Surtel & trim(DrumNo) & trim(itemkod)
				D7LB.REFTEXT501 = YaduBarkod
			End if

			d7LB.FORMKODU = EtiketFormu
			d7LB.STATU = "OK"
			d7LB.APPKODU = "STOK20"
			d7LB.EVRAKTYPE = "MG"
			d7LB.EVRAKNO = S20E.EVRAKNO

			Doc1.Save_Voucher
			Set d7LB2 = Doc1.Select2("___D7KIDSLB1", "D7KIDSLB", "", "", -1)
			d7LB1.AppendTable(d7LB2)
		End if
	Next
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
	Set STOK20_EtiketYarat = d7LB1
End Function 
Function STOK20_EtiketYarat2(EtiketFormu)
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")
	Set d7LB = Doc1.GetTableObject("D7KIDSLB")

	Doc1.CreateTableObject "xxx11dw=D7KIDSLB", "TMPCOPY"

	Set S20T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20T")
	Set S20E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20E")

	Set STOK20_EtiketYarat2 = d7LB1

	S20T.SafePack()
	if trim(S20T.KOD) = "" Then Exit Function

	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	For i = 1 to S20T.GetRowCount()

			BarkodNo = S20T.SERINO(i)
			Set d = Doc.Select1("de456789000", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Doc.LeftJustify(trim(BarkodNo), 30) & "'", "", -1)
			if d.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
				Doc1.Load_Voucher BarkodNo

				d7LB.SKOD = S20T.KOD(i)
				d7LB.LOTNUMBER = S20T.LOTNUMBER(i)
				d7LB.SERINO = S20T.SERINO(i)
				d7LB.SF_MIKTAR = S20T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
				d7LB.SF_UNIT = S20T.SF_SF_UNIT(i)

				d7LB.MPSTM_TRNUM = S20T.MPSTM_TRNUM(i)
				d7LB.FOKOD = S20T.FOKOD(i)
				d7LB.AMBCODE1 = S20E.AMBCODE
				d7LB.AMBCODE2 = S20E.IMALATAMBCODE
				d7LB.AMBCODE3 = S20E.YANMAMULAMBCODE
				d7LB.REFTARIH1 = S20E.TARIH
				d7LB.CH_MUSTERI = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10TM", "MUSTERIKODU", S20T.MPSNO(i) & S20T.MPSTM_TRNUM(i), "EVRAKNO+TRNUM")

				d7LB.FORMKODU = EtiketFormu
				d7LB.STATU = "OK"
				d7LB.APPKODU = "STOK20"
				d7LB.EVRAKTYPE = "MG"
				d7LB.EVRAKNO = S20E.EVRAKNO

			Doc1.Save_Voucher
			Set d7LB2 = Doc1.Select2("___D7KIDSLB1", "D7KIDSLB", "", "", -1)
			d7LB1.AppendTable(d7LB2)
		End if
	Next
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
	Set STOK20_EtiketYarat2 = d7LB1
End Function 

Function STOK26_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")
	Set d7LB = Doc1.GetTableObject("D7KIDSLB")

	Doc1.CreateTableObject "xxx11dw=D7KIDSLB", "TMPCOPY"
	Set d7LB1 = Doc1.GetTableObject("xxx11dw")

	Set S26T = Doc.Select2g("def","STOK26T","","LOCATION_NEW1+LOCATION_NEW2+LOCATION_NEW3+LOCATION_NEW4","KOD+LOCATION_NEW1+LOCATION_NEW2+LOCATION_NEW3+LOCATION_NEW4",-1)
	Set S26E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK26E")

	Set STOK26_EtiketYarat = d7LB1


	S26T.SafePack()
	if trim(S26T.KOD) = "" Then Exit Function

	For i = 1 to S26T.GetRowCount()

		BarkodNo = Doc.Sayac0danAl("SERINO")
		Set d = Doc.Select1("de456789000", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Doc.LeftJustify(trim(BarkodNo), 30) & "'", "", -1)
		if d.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
			Doc1.Load_Voucher BarkodNo

			d7LB.SKOD = S26T.KOD(i)
			d7LB.LOTNUMBER = S26T.LOTNUMBER(i)
			d7LB.SERINO = S26T.SERINO(i)
			d7LB.SF_MIKTAR = 1
			d7LB.SF_UNIT = S26T.SF_SF_UNIT(i)
			d7LB.FOKOD = S26T.FOKOD(i)
			d7LB.AMBCODE1 = S26E.AMBCODE
			d7LB.FORMKODU = EtiketFormu
			d7LB.STATU = "OK"
			d7LB.APPKODU = "STOK26"
			d7LB.EVRAKTYPE = "TR"
			d7LB.EVRAKNO = S26E.EVRAKNO
			d7LB.EANORDUPLICATE = "1"

			Doc1.Save_Voucher

			d7LB.LOCATION1 = S26T.LOCATION_NEW1(i)
			d7LB.LOCATION2 = S26T.LOCATION_NEW2(i)
			d7LB.LOCATION3 = S26T.LOCATION_NEW3(i)
			d7LB.LOCATION4 = S26T.LOCATION_NEW4(i)
			

			Set d7LB2 = Doc1.Select2("___D7KIDSLB1", "D7KIDSLB", "", "", -1)
			d7LB1.AppendTable(d7LB2)

			d7LB.LOCATION1 = ""
			d7LB.LOCATION2 = ""
			d7LB.LOCATION3 = ""
			d7LB.LOCATION4 = ""
		End if
	Next
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
	Set STOK26_EtiketYarat = d7LB1
End Function 



Function STOK21_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")
	Set d7LB = Doc1.GetTableObject("D7KIDSLB")

	Doc1.CreateTableObject "xxx11dw=D7KIDSLB", "TMPCOPY"
	Set d7LB1 = Doc1.GetTableObject("xxx11dw")
	Set STOK21_EtiketYarat = d7LB1

	Set S21T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK21T")
	Set S21E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK21E")

	BarkodNo = Doc.LeftJustify(trim(S21T.SERINO), 30)
	if trim(BarkodNo) = "" Then Exit Function


	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("dr5t67878j", "STOK01", "SERINO='" & S21T.SERINO & "'", "", -1)
	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function

	S01.GroupBy("SERINO")
	Doc1.Load_Voucher BarkodNo
	d7LB.SF_MIKTAR = d7LB.SF_MIKTAR - (d7LB.SF_BAKIYE - S01.SF_MIKTAR)

	Doc1.Save_Voucher
	Set d7LB2 = Doc1.Select2("___D7KIDSLB1", "D7KIDSLB", "", "", -1)
	d7LB1.AppendTable(d7LB2)
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
	Set STOK21_EtiketYarat = d7LB1
End Function 
Function MMPS10_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")
	Set d7LB = Doc1.GetTableObject("D7KIDSLB")

	Doc1.CreateTableObject "xxx11dw=D7KIDSLB", "TMPCOPY"
	Set d7LB1 = Doc1.GetTableObject("xxx11dw")
	
	Set M10TM = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10TM")
	Set M10E = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10E")
	Set M10T = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10T")
	M10TM.SafePack()
	For j = 1 to M10TM.GetRowCount()
		Miktar = M10TM.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(j)
		'DefKutuIciMiktar = GetStdKoliAdet(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(j), M10TM.MUSTERIKODU(j), "")
		KutuIciMiktar = Doc.GetUDFValue("MMPS10E", M10E.EVRAKNO, "KOLI_MIK",0)
		KutuIciMiktar = doc.Round(KutuIciMiktar,0)
		LotNo = Doc.GetUDFValue("MMPS10E", M10E.EVRAKNO, "LOTNO",0)

		'EtkTarih = Doc.GetUDFValue("MMPS10E", M10E.EVRAKNO, "ETK_TRH",0)

		'if KutuIciMiktar = 0 then KutuIciMiktar = DefKutuIciMiktar
		'if KutuIciMiktar = 0 then KutuIciMiktar = Miktar
		EtiketAdedi = Doc.GetUDFValue("MMPS10E", M10E.EVRAKNO, "ETIK_ADT",0)	
		if EtiketAdedi = 0 Then EtiketAdedi = 1

		for i = 1 to EtiketAdedi
			Redim IDArray(1,0)
			KutuMiktari = KutuIciMiktar

			BarkodNo = Doc.Sayac0danAl("SERINO")
			Doc1.Load_Voucher BarkodNo

			d7LB.SKOD = M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(j)
			d7LB.LOTNUMBER = LotNo
			d7LB.SERINO = trim(BarkodNo)
			d7LB.SF_MIKTAR = KutuMiktari
			d7LB.SF_UNIT = M10TM.SF_SF_UNIT(j)
			d7LB.MPSNO = M10E.EVRAKNO
			d7LB.FOKOD = M10TM.FOKOD_MAMULKODU(j)
			d7LB.MPSTM_TRNUM = M10TM.TRNUM(j)

			d7LB.REFTARIH1 = Doc.BuGun()

			CHKodu = M10TM.MUSTERIKODU(j)
			if trim(CHKodu) = "" Then
				CHKodu = GetMusteriCHKodu(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(j))
			End if
			d7LB.CH_MUSTERI = CHKodu
			d7LB.FORMKODU = EtiketFormu
			d7LB.STATU = ""
			d7LB.APPKODU = "MMPS10"
			d7LB.EVRAKTYPE = "UP"
			d7LB.EVRAKNO = M10E.EVRAKNO
			d7LB.GK_1 = "STOK"
			d7LB.REFTEXT02 = GetSonOp(M10T)
			SonIs = GetSonIs(M10T)
			d7LB.REFTEXT03 = GetSonrakiOp(M10T, SonIs)
			Doc1.Save_Voucher

Function TRACK25_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Set STC = Doc.GetTableObject("STACK")
	Set S25T = Doc.Select1("de34frt5454xx", "STOK25T", "EVRAKNO='" & STC.SON_EVRAKNO & "'", "", -1)
	Doc.CreateTableObject "xxx11dw=D7KIDSLB", "TMPCOPY"
	Set d7LB1 = Doc.GetTableObject("xxx11dw")

	For i = 1 to S25T.GetRowCount()
		if (S25T.KOD(i)) <> "" Then
			Set d7LB = Doc.Select1("de444_dd", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & S25T.NEWSERINO(i) & "'", "", -1)
			if d7LB.GetRowCount() > 0 Then d7LB1.AppendTable(d7LB)
			EskiEtiket = D7LB.ILKETIKET
		End if
	Next
	if trim(EskiEtiket) <> "" Then
		Set d7LB = Doc.Select1("de444_ddV", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & EskiEtiket & "'", "", -1)
		if d7LB.GetRowCount() > 0 Then d7LB1.AppendTable(d7LB)
	End if

	Set TRACK25_EtiketYarat = d7LB1
End Function
Function STOK25_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Set S25E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK25E")
	Set S25T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK25T")

	S25T.SafePack()

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")
	Set d7LB = Doc1.GetTableObject("D7KIDSLB")

	Doc1.CreateTableObject "xxx11dw=D7KIDSLB", "TMPCOPY"
	Set d7LB1 = Doc1.GetTableObject("xxx11dw")

	For i = 1 to S25T.GetRowCount()
		SeriNo = S25T.NEWSERINO(i)
		Barkod = Doc.LeftJustify(trim(SeriNo), 30)
		Set D7LB_111 = Doc.Select1("dee3ffghg7y66", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Barkod & "'", "", -1)
		if trim(Barkod) <> "" and D7LB_111.GetRowCount = 0 Then
			Doc1.Load_Voucher SeriNo
	
			d7LB.SKOD = S25T.NEWCODE(i)
			d7LB.APPKODU = "STOK25"
			d7LB.EVRAKTYPE = "KD"
			d7LB.EVRAKNO = S25E.EVRAKNO
			d7LB.LOTNUMBER = S25T.NEWLOTNUMBER(i)
			d7LB.SF_MIKTAR = S25T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			d7LB.SF_UNIT = S25T.OSF_SF_UNIT(i)
			d7LB.VRI_NUM1 = S25T.PN_SF_VRI_NUM1(i)
			d7LB.VRI_NUM2 = S25T.PN_SF_VRI_NUM2(i)
			d7LB.FOKOD = S25T.NEWFOKOD(i)
			d7LB.VRI_VR_C = S25T.PN_SF_VRI_VR_C(i)
			d7LB.VRI_VR_R1 = S25T.PN_SF_VRI_VR_R1(i)
			d7LB.VRI_VR_R2 = S25T.PN_SF_VRI_VR_R2(i)
			d7LB.VRI_VR_R3 = S25T.PN_SF_VRI_VR_R3(i)

			OncekiBarkod = Doc.LeftJustify(trim(S25T.SERINO(i)), 30)
			Set D7LB_Eski = Doc.Select1("dee3ff44ghg7y66", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & OncekiBarkod & "'", "", -1)

			if D7LB_Eski.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
				d7LB.ONCEKETIKET = OncekiBarkod
				d7LB.ILKETIKET = OncekiBarkod
				d7LB.CH_TEDARIKCI = D7LB_Eski.CH_TEDARIKCI
				d7LB.SIPARISNO_46 = D7LB_Eski.SIPARISNO_46
				d7LB.SIPARTNO_46 = D7LB_Eski.SIPARTNO_46
				d7LB.CH_MUSTERI = D7LB_Eski.CH_MUSTERI
				d7LB.FORMKODU = D7LB_Eski.FORMKODU
				d7LB.STATU = D7LB_Eski.STATU
			Else
				FG_Array = GetFasonInfo(S25T.KOD(i), S25T.LOTNUMBER(i), S25T.SERINO(i))
				d7LB.CH_TEDARIKCI = FG_Array(0)
				d7LB.SIPARISNO_46 = FG_Array(1)
				d7LB.SIPARTNO_46 = FG_Array(2)
				d7LB.FORMKODU = EtiketFormu
			End if
			d7LB.AMBCODE1 = S25E.TARGETAMBCODE
			
			Doc1.Save_Voucher

			Set d7LB2 = Doc1.Select2("___D7KIDSLB1", "D7KIDSLB", "", "", -1)
			d7LB1.AppendTable(d7LB2)
		Else
			d7LB1.AppendTable(D7LB_111)
			OncekiBarkod = Doc.LeftJustify(trim(S25T.SERINO(i)), 30)
			Set D7LB_Eski = Doc.Select1("dee3ff44ghg7y66", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & OncekiBarkod & "'", "", -1)
			if D7LB_Eski.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
				d7LB1.AppendTable(D7LB_Eski)
			End if
		End if
	Next
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	Set STOK25_EtiketYarat = d7LB1
End Function

Function TRACKIZ_EtiketYarat(EtiketFormu)
	Doc.CreateTableObject "xxx11dw=D7KIDSLB", "TMPCOPY"
	Set d7LB1 = Doc.GetTableObject("xxx11dw")
	Set d7LB  = Doc.GetTableObject("D7KIDSLB")
	Set d7ST = Doc.GetTableObject("D7KIDSLB_ST")
	Set STCK = Doc.GetTableObject("STACK_ST")
	Set TRACKIZ_EtiketYarat = d7LB1

	IsNo = d7ST.JOBNO
	if trim(IsNo) = "" or trim(d7ST.ISMERKEZI) = "" Then 
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Ýþ No ve Tezgah barkodlarýný okutmalýsýnýz", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Function
	End if

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK20", "")
	KutuIciMiktar = InputBox("Kasa Ýçi Adedi Giriniz")
	KutuIciMiktar = Doc.CDbl(trim(KutuIciMiktar))

	if KutuIciMiktar = 0 Then Exit Function
	
	Set M10TM = Doc.Select1("dde4434f556", "MMPS10TM", "EVRAKNO='" & Left(IsNo, 10) & "'", "", -1)
	Set M10T = Doc.Select1("dde4434f556MM", "MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO='" & Left(IsNo, 10) & "'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='I'", "", -1)
	For j = 1 to M10TM.GetRowCount()
		LotNo = Mid(Replace(Doc.BuGun, "/", ""), 3) & "-" & trim(M10TM.EVRAKNO)
		EtiketAdedi = 1

		for i = 1 to EtiketAdedi
			KutuMiktari = KutuIciMiktar

			BarkodNo = Doc.Sayac0danAl("SERINO")
			Doc.Load_Voucher BarkodNo

			d7LB.SKOD = M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(j)
			d7LB.LOTNUMBER = LotNo
			d7LB.SERINO = trim(BarkodNo)
			d7LB.SF_MIKTAR = KutuMiktari
			d7LB.SF_UNIT = M10TM.SF_SF_UNIT(j)
			d7LB.MPSNO = M10TM.EVRAKNO(j)
			d7LB.FOKOD = M10TM.FOKOD_MAMULKODU(j)
			d7LB.MPSTM_TRNUM = M10TM.TRNUM(j)

			d7LB.REFTARIH1 = Doc.BuGun()

			CHKodu = M10TM.MUSTERIKODU(j)
			if trim(CHKodu) = "" Then
				CHKodu = GetMusteriCHKodu(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(j))
			End if
			d7LB.CH_MUSTERI = CHKodu
			d7LB.FORMKODU = EtiketFormu
			d7LB.STATU = "OK"
			d7LB.APPKODU = "MMPS10"
			d7LB.EVRAKTYPE = "UP"
			d7LB.EVRAKNO = M10TM.EVRAKNO
			SonIs = GetSonIs(M10T)
			if trim(d7ST.JOBNO) = trim(SonIs) Then d7LB.GK_1 = "STOK"
			d7LB.REFTEXT02 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "R_OPERASYON", d7ST.JOBNO, "JOBNO")
			d7LB.REFTEXT03 = GetSonrakiOp(M10T, d7ST.JOBNO)
			Doc.Save_Voucher
			Set d7LB2 = Doc.Select2("___D7KIDSLB1", "D7KIDSLB", "", "", -1)
			d7LB1.AppendTable(d7LB2)
		Next
	Next
	Doc.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
	Set TRACKIZ_EtiketYarat = d7LB1
	d7LB1.SafePack()
	STCK.D_ID = d7LB1.SERINO
	if trim(IsNo) = trim(SonIs) Then Call UretimFisiOlustur(Doc1, d7LB1)
End Function

		Barkod = Doc.LeftJustify(trim(MTableT2.EDEFF__ETIKET_NO(i)), 30)
		Set D7LB_111 = Doc.Select1("dee3ffghg7y66", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Barkod & "'", "", -1)
		if trim(Barkod) <> "" and D7LB_111.GetRowCount = 0 Then
			Doc1.Load_Voucher Barkod
	
			d7LB.SKOD = MTableT2.KOD(i)
			d7LB.APPKODU = "SAYM01"
			d7LB.EVRAKTYPE = "EF"
			d7LB.EVRAKNO = S01E.EVRAKNO
			d7LB.LOTNUMBER = MTableT2.LOTNUMBER(i)
			d7LB.SERINO = MTableT2.SERINO(i)
			d7LB.SF_MIKTAR = MTableT2.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			d7LB.SF_UNIT = MTableT2.SF_SF_UNIT(i)
			d7LB.VRI_NUM1 = MTableT2.SF_VRI_NUM1(i)
			d7LB.VRI_NUM2 = MTableT2.SF_VRI_NUM2(i)
			d7LB.FOKOD = MTableT2.FOKOD(i)
			d7LB.VRI_VR_C = MTableT2.SF_VRI_VR_C(i)
			d7LB.VRI_VR_R1 = MTableT2.SF_VRI_VR_R1(i)
			d7LB.VRI_VR_R2 = MTableT2.SF_VRI_VR_R2(i)
			d7LB.VRI_VR_R3 = MTableT2.SF_VRI_VR_R3(i)
			d7LB.AMBCODE1 = MTableT2.AMBCODE(i)
			d7LB.LOCATION1 = MTableT2.LOCATION1(i)
			d7LB.LOCATION2 = MTableT2.LOCATION2(i)
			d7LB.LOCATION3 = MTableT2.LOCATION3(i)
			d7LB.LOCATION4 = MTableT2.LOCATION4(i)
			d7LB.CH_TEDARIKCI = ""
			d7LB.SIPARISNO_46 = ""
			d7LB.SIPARTNO_46 = ""
			d7LB.FORMKODU = EtiketFormu
			d7LB.REFTARIH1 = S01E.TARIH
			d7LB.STATU = "OK"
			Doc1.Save_Voucher

			Set d7LB2 = Doc1.Select2("___D7KIDSLB1", "D7KIDSLB", "", "", -1)
			d7LB1.AppendTable(d7LB2)
		Else
			d7LB1.AppendTable(D7LB_111)
		End if
	Next
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	Set SAYM01_EtiketYarat = d7LB1
End Function
Function GetFasonInfo(Kod, LotNo, SeriNo)
	Dim FasonArr(2)
	QStr = "KOD='" & Kod & "'&LOTNUMBER='" & LotNo & "'&SERINO='" & SeriNo & "'"
	Set S68T = Doc.Select1("deefft66", "STOK68T", QStr, "", -1)
	if S68T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		FasonArr(0) = S68T.CHCODE
		FasonArr(1) = S68T.SIPNO
		FasonArr(2) = S68T.SIPARTNO
	End if
	GetFasonInfo = FasonArr
End Function
Function GetMusteriCHKodu(StokKodu)
	Set S0GT = Doc.Select1("d3r4gh77u", "STOK0GT", "STOKCODE='" & StokKodu & "'", "", -1)
	if S0GT.GetRowCount() = 1 Then
		GetMusteriCHKodu = S0GT.KOD
	End if
End Function
Function GetSonrakiOp(M10TX, IsNo)
	SrNum = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "SRNUM", IsNo, "JOBNO")
	Set M10T1 = Doc.Select2("ded44hh7jx", M10TX.GetAppName, "R_KAYNAKTYPE='I'&SRNUM>'" & SrNum & "'", "SRNUM", -1)
	if M10T1.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		GetSonrakiOp = M10T1.R_OPERASYON(1)
	Else
		GetSonrakiOp = GetSonrakiUrunIlkOp(M10TX.R_MAMULKODU)
	End if
End Function
Function GetSonrakiUrunIlkOp(StokKodu)
	Set B01T = Doc.Select1("de3333r5t", "BOMU01T", "AKTIF_PASIF='*'&BOMREC_INPUTTYPE='H'&BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE='" & StokKodu & "'", "", -1)
	if B01T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		Set B01T1 = Doc.Select1("d44f5", "BOMU01T", "EVRAKNO='" & B01T.EVRAKNO & "'&BOMREC_INPUTTYPE='I'", "SRNUM", 1)
		if B01T1.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			GetSonrakiUrunIlkOp = B01T1.BOMREC_OPERASYON
		End if
	End if
End Function

	if M10TI.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		GetSonOp = M10TI.R_OPERASYON
	End if
End Function
Function GetSonIs(M10T)

	if M10TI.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		GetSonIs = M10TI.JOBNO
	End if
End Function
Sub UretimFisiOlustur(Doc1, d7LB1)
	Set S20E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK20E")
	Set S20T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK20T")

	Set S20T1 = Doc.Select1("dd33f56jjj77", "STOK20T", "MPSNO='" & d7LB1.MPSNO & "'&TARIH='" & Doc.BuGun & "'", "", -1)
	if S20T1.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		if Doc1.Load_Voucher(S20T1.EVRAKNO) <> 0 Then
			Doc1.New_Voucher
			if Left(D7LB1.SKOD, 1) = "3" Then
				S20E.AMBCODE = "MA"
			Else
				S20E.AMBCODE = "IM"
			End if
			S20E.IMALATAMBCODE = "IM"
			S20E.YANMAMULAMBCODE = "IM"
		End if
	Else
		Doc1.New_Voucher
		if Left(D7LB1.SKOD, 1) = "3" Then
			S20E.AMBCODE = "MA"
		Else
			S20E.AMBCODE = "IM"
		End if
		S20E.IMALATAMBCODE = "IM"
		S20E.YANMAMULAMBCODE = "IM"
	End if		

	For i = 1 to d7LB1.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = S20T.AddRow()
		S20T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		S20T.MPSNO = d7LB1.MPSNO(i)
		if trim(S20T.MPSTM_TRNUM) = "" Then S20T.MPSTM_TRNUM = d7LB1.MPSTM_TRNUM(i)
		S20T.LOTNUMBER = d7LB1.LOTNUMBER(i)
		S20T.SERINO = d7LB1.SERINO(i)
		S20T.FOKOD = d7LB1.FOKOD(i)
		S20T.SF_MIKTAR = D7LB1.SF_MIKTAR(i)
	Next
	Doc1.RunCommand "CMD_SIRALIK_ISLEM_003"
	Doc1.RunCommand "CMD_STOK01DEBULUNDUMU_BILGISATIRIE"

	Doc1.Save_Voucher()

	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
End Sub
Function GetMakaraMiktar()
	GetMakaraMiktar = 0
	Set SFDC31E = Doc.GetTableObject("SFDC31E")
	Set S31E = Doc.Select1G("sdfg4gdry", "SFDC31E", "JOBNO='" & SFDC31E.JOBNO & "'&LOTNUMBER='" & SFDC31E.LOTNUMBER & "'&D7_ISLEM_KODU='U'", "", "LOTNUMBER", -1)
	if S31E.GetRowCount() > 0 Then GetMakaraMiktar = S31E.SF_MIKTAR
End Function

----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : ER2011 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : FL-STML-ER
Hangi Tablo için : STOK48
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Fiyat listesini standart maliyet tablosuna aktarma
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	EndeksKodu = GKYoksaEkle("STMTYP", "YM", "Maliyet Endeksi")
	UnsurKodu = GKYoksaEkle("STMMUS", "MB", "Mal Bedeli")

	STOK48T.SafePack()

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STDMH0","")
	Set STDMH0E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STDMH0E")
	Set STDMH0T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STDMH0T")
	For i = 1 to STOK48T.GetRecCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Aktarýlýyor : " & i & " / " & STOK48T.GetRecCount()

		StokKodu = STOK48T.KOD(i)
		Set ST = Doc.Select1("ww", "STDMH0E","KOD='" & StokKodu & "'&STDMALTYPE='" & EndeksKodu & "'", "", -1)
		if ST.GetRowCount() = 0 then
'			ret = Doc1.New_Voucher()				
'			STDMH0E.STDMALTYPE = EndeksKodu
'			STDMH0E.KOD = StokKodu
'			STDMH0T.AddRow
'			satirNo = STDMH0T.GetRecCount
'			Tarih = "01/01/2009"
		else
			ret = Doc1.Load_Voucher(ST.EVRAKNO)
			if ret <> 0 then
				k = Doc.d7MsgBox(trim(StokKodu) & ": Standart Maliyet tablosu yüklenemedi. Ýþlem kesilmiþtir.","dinamo",MB_OK)
				exit sub
			end if
			satirNo = satirBul(STDMH0T, UnsurKodu)
			if satirNo = 0 then
				satirNo = STDMH0T.AddRow
				Tarih = "01/01/2009"
			Else
				Tarih = STDMH0T.VALIDAFTERTARIH(satirNo)
			End if
			Fiyat = STOK48T.PRICE(i)
			IslemBirimi = STOK48T.SF_UNIT(i)
			TemelBirim = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", STOK48T.KOD(i), "")
			TemelFiyat = Doc.Stok00_toUnit(STOK48T.KOD(i), TemelBirim, IslemBirimi, d7c_ErrMsgNo) * Fiyat

			STDMH0T.UNSUR(satirNo) = UnsurKodu
			STDMH0T.TUTAR(satirNo) = TemelFiyat
			STDMH0T.PARABIRIMI(satirNo) = STOK48T.PRICEUNIT(i)
			STDMH0T.VALIDAFTERTARIH(satirNo) = Tarih
			ret = Doc1.Save_Voucher()
			if ret <> 0 then
				k = Doc.d7MsgBox(trim(StokKodu) & ": Standart Maliyet tablosu kaydedilemedi. Ýþlem kesilmiþtir.","dinamo",MB_OK)
				ret = Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
				exit sub
			end if
			ret = Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
		end if
	Next
End Sub
Function satirBul(STDMH0T, UnsurKodu)
	satirBul = 0
	For i = 1 to STDMH0T.GetRowCount()
		if trim(STDMH0T.UNSUR(i)) = trim(UnsurKodu) then
			satirBul = i
			exit function
		end if
	Next
End Function
Function GKYoksaEkle(GrupKoduEvrakNo, GrupKodu, Ad)
	Set GST = Doc.Select1("ddee", "GECOUST", "EVRAKNO='" & GrupKoduEvrakNo & "'&KOD='" & GrupKodu & "'", "", -1)
	if GST.GetRowCount = 0 Then
		Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("GECO10", "")
		Set GT = Doc1.GetTableObject("GECOUST")

		Doc1.Load_Voucher GrupKoduEvrakNo
		satirNo = GT.AddRow
		GT.KOD(satirNo) = GrupKodu
		GT.AD(satirNo) = Ad
		Doc1.Save_Voucher
		Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	End if
	GKYoksaEkle = GrupKodu
End Function

Sub Makro1()
set tblDntask1k = doc.GetTableObject("DNTASK1K")

dim i
for i=1 to tblDntask1k.GetRecCount()
	Select Case Trim(tblDntask1k.FIELDNAME1(i))
		Case "TARIH"
			tblDntask1k.DEFVALUE01 = Doc.StrDate_Increment(Doc.BuGun(),-8)
			tblDntask1k.DEFVALUE02 = Doc.StrDate_Increment(Doc.BuGun(),-1)
	End Select
Next

tblDntask1k = Nothing
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	MPSNo = InputBox("Rezervasyonu iptal edilecek MPS numarasýný giriniz")
	if trim(MPSNo) = "" Then Exit Sub
	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("dfwr3gdfg", "STOK01", "ALLOC_MPSEVNO='" & trim(MPSNo) & "'", "", -1)
	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Stokta " & trim(MPSNo) & " no.lu MPS için rezervasyon bulunamadý", "", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	if trim(STOK83E.EVRAKNO) = "" Then
		Doc.New_Voucher
		STOK83E.AMBCODE = S01.AMBCODE(1)
		STOK83E.TARGETAMBCODE = S01.AMBCODE(1)
	End if
	STOK83E.MPSGIRISCIKISTYPE = "-"
	STOK83T.SafePack

	For i = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = STOK83T.AddRow
		STOK83T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		STOK83T.MPSNO = S01.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(i)
		STOK83T.JOBNO = S01.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(i)
		STOK83T.ALANAMBARDASREZ = "H"
		STOK83T.KOD = S01.KOD(i)
		STOK83T.LOTNUMBER = S01.LOTNUMBER(i)
		STOK83T.SF_MIKTAR = S01.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		STOK83T.SF_SF_UNIT = S01.SF_UNIT(i)
		STOK83T.SERINO = S01.SERINO(i)
		STOK83T.SF_VRI_VR_C = S01.VRI_VR_C(i)
		STOK83T.SF_VRI_VR_R1 = S01.VRI_VR_R1(i)
		STOK83T.SF_VRI_VR_R2 = S01.VRI_VR_R2(i)
		STOK83T.SF_VRI_VR_R3 = S01.VRI_VR_R3(i)
		STOK83T.SF_VRI_NUM1 = S01.VRI_NUM1(i)
		STOK83T.SF_VRI_NUM2 = S01.VRI_NUM2(i)
		STOK83T.FOKOD = S01.FOKOD(i)
		STOK83T.AMBCODE_TR = S01.AMBCODE(i)
		STOK83T.TARGETAMBCODE_TR = S01.AMBCODE(i)
		STOK83T.LOCATION1 = S01.LOCATION1(i)
		STOK83T.LOCATION2 = S01.LOCATION2(i)
		STOK83T.LOCATION3 = S01.LOCATION3(i)
		STOK83T.LOCATION4 = S01.LOCATION4(i)
		STOK83T.LOCATION_NEW1 = S01.LOCATION1(i)
		STOK83T.LOCATION_NEW2 = S01.LOCATION2(i)
		STOK83T.LOCATION_NEW3 = S01.LOCATION3(i)
		STOK83T.LOCATION_NEW4 = S01.LOCATION4(i)
		STOK83T.OALLOC_SYSSTATUS = S01.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(i)
		STOK83T.OALLOC_SIPTYPE = S01.ALLOC_SIPTYPE(i)
		STOK83T.OALLOC_EVRAKTYPE = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(i)
		STOK83T.OALLOC_EVRAKNO = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(i)

----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : ER2011 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : SAYIM-01
Hangi Tablo için : SY99PO

Dim S01Arr, S01Arr1
Sub Makro1()
	if SayimYapilanDepolardaRezervasyonVar Then Exit Sub

	S01Arr = Array("KOD", "LOTNUMBER", "SERINO", "LOCATION1", "LOCATION2", "LOCATION3", "LOCATION4", "FOKOD", _
					"SF_VRI_VR_RC", "SF_VRI_VR_R1", "SF_VRI_VR_R2", "SF_VRI_VR_R3", "SF_VRI_NUM1", "SF_VRI_NUM2", "SF_SF_UNIT")
	S01Arr1 = Array("KOD", "LOTNUMBER", "SERINO", "LOCATION1", "LOCATION2", "LOCATION3", "LOCATION4", "FOKOD", _
					"VRI_VR_RC", "VRI_VR_R1", "VRI_VR_R2", "VRI_VR_R3", "VRI_NUM1", "VRI_NUM2", "SF_UNIT")

	Call GirisCikisFisleriOlustur()

	Doc.d7MsgBox "Ýkinci makroyu çalýþtýrmadan önce Süz butonuna tekrar basmayý unutmayýnýz", "Dikkat", MB_OK+MB_ICONWARNING
End Sub
Sub GirisCikisFisleriOlustur()
	Set S99 = Doc.Select2("_S99", "SAYM99T", "", "KOD", -1)
	S99.GroupBy("KOD")
	Set S99G = Doc.Select2("_S99G", "_S99", "SF_FARK>0", "", -1)
	Set S99C = Doc.Select2("_S99C", "_S99", "SF_FARK<0", "", -1)

	if S99C.GetRowCount() > 0 Then 
		if CikisFisiniOlustur(S99C) <> 0 Then Exit Sub
	End if
	if S99G.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		if GirisFisiniOlustur(S99G) = 0 Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Ýþlem Baþarýyla Tamamlandý", "", MB_OK
		End if
	End If
End Sub
Function CikisFisiniOlustur(S99C)
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentUI("STOK21", "")
	Set S21E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK21E")	
	Set S21T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK21T")	
	Doc1.New_Voucher()
	S21E.AMBCODE = GetDepoKodu
	S21E.NITELIK = "SAYIM"

	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("_S01", "STOK01", "KOD='ssw4444bvhgf'", "", -1)
	For i = 1 to S99C.GetRowCount()
		CikacakMiktar = Doc.DRound(S99C.SF_FARK(i))
		if Doc.Round(CikacakMiktar,3) < 0 Then
			S01.Empty
			DepoStr = SAYM99E.DEPO1 & SAYM99E.DEPO2 & SAYM99E.DEPO3 & SAYM99E.DEPO4 & SAYM99E.DEPO5 & SAYM99E.DEPO6 & _
							SAYM99E.DEPO7 & SAYM99E.DEPO8 & SAYM99E.DEPO9 & SAYM99E.DEPO10 & SAYM99E.DEPO11 & SAYM99E.DEPO12
			if trim(DepoStr) = "" Then
				Set S99P = Doc.Select2("xegt6", "SAYM99T", "", "", -1)
				S99P.GroupBy("AMBCODE")
				For ni = 1 to S99P.GetRowCount()
					DepoKodu = S99P.AMBCODE(ni)
					if trim(DepoKodu) <> "" Then
						DepoStr = "'&AMBCODE='" & DepoKodu & "'"
						Set S01X = Doc.Select1("_S01X", "STOK01", "KOD='" & S99C.KOD(i) & DepoStr, "", -1)
						S01.AppendTable(S01X)
					End if
				Next
			Else
				For ni = 1 to 12
					DepoKodu = SAYM99E.GetFieldValue("DEPO" & ni, 1)
					if trim(DepoKodu) <> "" Then
						DepoStr = "'&AMBCODE='" & DepoKodu & "'"
						Set S01X = Doc.Select1("_S01X", "STOK01", "KOD='" & S99C.KOD(i) & DepoStr, "", -1)
						S01.AppendTable(S01X)
					End if
				Next
			End if
			KalanMiktar = Abs(CikacakMiktar)
			For k = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()
				if S01.SF_MIKTAR(k) > KalanMiktar Then
					CikanMiktar = KalanMiktar
				Else
					CikanMiktar = S01.SF_MIKTAR(k)
				End if
				KalanMiktar = KalanMiktar - CikanMiktar
				satirNo = S21T.AddRow()
				S21T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
				For j = 0 to uBound(S01Arr)
					AlanAdi = S01Arr(j)
					S01AlanAdi = S01Arr1(j)
					S01Deger = S01.GetFieldValue(S01AlanAdi, k)
					S21T.SetFieldValue AlanAdi, satirNo, S01Deger
				Next
				S21T.CIKAN_MIKTAR(satirNo) = Doc.DRound(CikanMiktar)
				S21T.AMBCODE_TR(satirNo) = S01.AMBCODE(k)
				if KalanMiktar = 0 Then Exit For				
			Next
		End if
	Next
	CikisFisiniOlustur = Doc1.Save_Voucher
	if CikisFisiniOlustur = 0 Then
		Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
		Doc1.RUN_APP_EXIT_OnIdle
	End if
End Function
Function GirisFisiniOlustur(S99G)
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentUI("STOK24", "")
	Set S24E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK24E")	
	Set S24T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK24T")	
	Doc1.New_Voucher()
	S24E.AMBCODE = GetDepoKodu
	S24E.NITELIK = "SAYIM"

	For i = 1 to S99G.GetRowCount()
		if Doc.DRound(S99G.SF_FARK(i)) > 0 Then
			satirNo = S24T.AddRow()
			S24T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			For j = 0 to uBound(S01Arr)
				AlanAdi = S01Arr(j)
				S99GDeger = S99G.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi, i)
				S24T.SetFieldValue AlanAdi, satirNo, S99GDeger
			Next
			S24T.GIREN_MIKTAR(satirNo) = Doc.DRound(S99G.SF_FARK(i))
		End if
	Next
	GirisFisiniOlustur = Doc1.Save_Voucher
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Function
Function SayimYapilanDepolardaRezervasyonVar()
	SayimYapilanDepolardaRezervasyonVar = False
	QStr = "SELECT ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE, ALLOC_EVRAKNO, KOD, Sum(SF_MIKTAR) SF_MIKTAR, SF_UNIT"
	QStr = QStr & " FROM STOK01 WHERE ALLOC_SYSSTATUS<>' '"
	AmbCodeStr = ""
	For i = 1 to 12
		AmbCodeStr = AmbCodeStr & SAYM99E.GetFieldValue("DEPO" & i, 1)
	Next
	if trim(AmbCodeStr) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND AMBCODE IN (' '"
		For i = 1 to 12
			if trim(SAYM99E.GetFieldValue("DEPO" & i, 1)) <> "" Then
				QStr = QStr & ", '" & SAYM99E.GetFieldValue("DEPO" & i, 1) & "'"
			End if
		Next
		QStr = QStr & ")"
	End if
	QStr = QStr & "GROUP BY ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE, ALLOC_EVRAKNO, KOD, SF_UNIT"

	Set S01 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdfw4gsd", QStr)
	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function

	Set f = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	Doc.d7MsgBox "Sayým yapýlan Depolarda Rezervasyonlar Var. Rezervasyonlar iptal edildikten sonra makroyu tekrar çalýþtýrýnýz", "", MB_OK
	Doc.d7MsgBox "Rezerve evrak no.larý ve stok kodlarý listesi :", "", MB_OK

	For i = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Evrak Türü-No : " & S01.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(i) & " - " & trim(S01.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(i)) & _
							", Stok Kodu : " & S01.KOD(i) & ", Miktar : " & S01.SF_MIKTAR(i) & " " & S01.SF_UNIT(i), "", MB_OK
		
	Next
	SayimYapilanDepolardaRezervasyonVar = True
End Function
Function GetDepoKodu()
	For i = 1 to 12
		Depo = SAYM99E.GetFieldValue("DEPO" & i,1)
		if trim(Depo) <> "" Then
			GetDepoKodu = Depo
			Exit Function
		End if
	Next
	For i = 1 to 20
		EvrakNo = SAYM99E.GetFieldValue("S_EVRAKNO" & i, 1)
		if trim(EvrakNo) <> "" Then
			Depo = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("SAYM11T", "AMBCODE", EvrakNo & "000001", "EVRAKNO+SRNUM")
			if trim(Depo) <> "" Then
				GetDepoKodu = Depo
				Exit Function
			End if
		End if
	Next
	Set G00 = Doc.Select1("Fr33", "GDEF00", "MRPDEYOK10<>'1'", "", -1)
	if G00.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		GetDepoKodu = G00.KOD(1)
	End if
End Function

Dim S01Arr, OldS01Arr, NewS01Arr
Sub Makro1()
	S01Arr = Array("KOD", "LOTNUMBER", "SERINO", "LOCATION1", "LOCATION2", "LOCATION3", "LOCATION4", "FOKOD", _
					"SF_VRI_VR_RC", "SF_VRI_VR_R1", "SF_VRI_VR_R2", "SF_VRI_VR_R3", "SF_VRI_NUM1", "SF_VRI_NUM2", "SF_SF_UNIT", "AMBCODE")

	OldS01Arr = Array("KOD", "LOTNUMBER", "SERINO", "LOCATION1", "LOCATION2", "LOCATION3", "LOCATION4", "FOKOD", _
					"OSF_VRI_VR_C", "OSF_VRI_VR_R1", "OSF_VRI_VR_R2", "OSF_VRI_VR_R3", "OSF_VRI_NUM1", "OSF_VRI_NUM2", "OSF_SF_UNIT", "AMBCODE_TR")


	NewS01Arr = Array("NEWCODE", "NEWLOTNUMBER", "NEWSERINO", "NEWLOCATION1", "NEWLOCATION2", "NEWLOCATION3", "NEWLOCATION4", "NEWFOKOD", _
					"PN_SF_VRI_VR_C", "PN_SF_VRI_VR_R1", "PN_SF_VRI_VR_R2", "PN_SF_VRI_VR_R3", "PN_SF_VRI_NUM1", "PN_SF_VRI_NUM2", "OSF_SF_UNIT", "TARGETAMBCODE_TR")

	Call KodLotSeriTransferFisiOlustur()
End Sub

Sub KodLotSeriTransferFisiOlustur()
	Set S99LISTE = Doc.Select2("_S99L", "SAYM99T", "SF_MIKTAR_01>0&SF_FARK<>0", "KOD", -1)
	Set S99SONUC = Doc.Select2("_S99S", "SAYM99T", "SF_MIKTAR_11>0&SF_FARK<>0", "KOD", -1)
	if S99LISTE.GetRowCount = 0 Then Exit Sub

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentUI("STOK25", "")
	Set S25E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25E")
	Set S25T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25T")

	Doc1.New_Voucher
	S25E.AMBCODE = S99LISTE.AMBCODE(1)
	S25E.TARGETAMBCODE = S99LISTE.AMBCODE(1)

	EskiKod = ""
	For i = 1 to S99LISTE.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Kod/Lot/Seri transfer fiþi yaratýlýyor : " & i & " / " & S99LISTE.GetRowCount()
		if S99LISTE.KOD(i) <> EskiKod Then
			Set SONUC = Doc.Select2("ddee", "_S99S", "KOD='" & S99LISTE.KOD(i) &"'", "", -1)
			DagitimSatir = 1
		End if
		ListeMiktar = S99LISTE.SF_MIKTAR_01(i)
		For j = DagitimSatir to SONUC.GetRowCount()
			Cikis = False
			SonucMiktar = SONUC.SF_MIKTAR_11(j)
			if SonucMiktar > ListeMiktar Then
				DagitilacakMiktar = ListeMiktar
				SONUC.SF_MIKTAR_11(j) = SONUC.SF_MIKTAR_11(j) - DagitilacakMiktar
				Cikis = True
			Else
				DagitilacakMiktar = SonucMiktar
				ListeMiktar = ListeMiktar - SonucMiktar
				SONUC.SF_MIKTAR_11(j) = 0
				DagitimSatir = DagitimSatir + 1
				if ListeMiktar = 0 Then Cikis = True
			End if

			satirNo = S25T.AddRow
			S25T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			For k = 0 to uBound(S01Arr)
				S25TOldAlan = OldS01Arr(k)
				S01Alan = S01Arr(k)
				S25T.SetFieldValue S25TOldAlan, satirNo, S99LISTE.GetFieldValue(S01Alan,i)
			Next
			S25T.SF_MIKTAR = DagitilacakMiktar

			For k = 0 to uBound(S01Arr)
				S25TNewAlan = NewS01Arr(k)
				S01Alan = S01Arr(k)
				S25T.SetFieldValue S25TNewAlan, satirNo, SONUC.GetFieldValue(S01Alan,j)
			Next

			if Cikis Then Exit For
		Next
		EskiKod = S99LISTE.KOD(i)
	Next
	Call EtkisizSatirlariSil(Doc1)
	'Call SendTableToExcel(S25T)
	if Doc1.Save_Voucher = 0 Then
		Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
		Doc1.RUN_APP_EXIT_OnIdle
	End if
End Sub

Sub EtkisizSatirlariSil(Doc1)
	Set S25T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25T")

	OldS01Arr = Array("KOD", "LOTNUMBER", "SERINO", "LOCATION1", "LOCATION2", "LOCATION3", "LOCATION4", "FOKOD", _
					"OSF_VRI_VR_C", "OSF_VRI_VR_R1", "OSF_VRI_VR_R2", "OSF_VRI_VR_R3", "OSF_VRI_NUM1", "OSF_VRI_NUM2", "OSF_SF_UNIT", "AMBCODE_TR")


	NewS01Arr = Array("NEWCODE", "NEWLOTNUMBER", "NEWSERINO", "NEWLOCATION1", "NEWLOCATION2", "NEWLOCATION3", "NEWLOCATION4", "NEWFOKOD", _
					"PN_SF_VRI_VR_C", "PN_SF_VRI_VR_R1", "PN_SF_VRI_VR_R2", "PN_SF_VRI_VR_R3", "PN_SF_VRI_NUM1", "PN_SF_VRI_NUM2", "OSF_SF_UNIT", "TARGETAMBCODE_TR")

	For i = 1 to S25T.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Etkisiz satýrlar siliniyor : " & i & " / " & S25T.GetRowCount()
		EskiDeger = ""
		YeniDeger = ""
		For k = 0 to uBound(OldS01Arr)
			S25TOldAlan = OldS01Arr(k)
			S25TNewAlan = NewS01Arr(k)
			EskiDeger = EskiDeger & S25T.GetFieldValue(S25TOldAlan, i)
			YeniDeger = YeniDeger & S25T.GetFieldValue(S25TNewAlan, i)
		Next
		if YeniDeger = EskiDeger Then
'etkisizSatir = etkisizSatir + 1
'			Doc.d7MsgBox i, "", MB_OK
			S25T.KOD(i) = ""
			S25T.OSF_SF_UNIT(i) = ""
		End if
		if Doc.DRound(S25T.SF_MIKTAR(i)) = 0 or Doc.DRound(S25T.OSF_STOK_MIKTAR(i)) = 0 Then
			S25T.KOD(i) = ""
			S25T.OSF_SF_UNIT(i) = ""
		End if
	Next
'msgbox etkisizSatir
	S25T.SafePack()
End Sub


Sub SendTableToExcel(Tablo)
	Set pe2 = Doc.CreateSObject("ExcelOutput")
	PE2.SetOrientation "P"
	PE2.PageB_ReportHead_std1 Tablo.GetAppName

	Alanlar = Tablo.GetFields()
	ncol = 0
	For i=0 To uBound(Alanlar,1)
		ncol = ncol+1
		if ncol > 256 Then Exit For
		AlanTuru = Alanlar(i,2)
			AlanAdi = Alanlar(i,0)
			If AlanTuru = "INT16" or AlanTuru = "INT32" or AlanTuru = "DOUBLE" Then
			Align = "RIGHT"
		Else
			Align = "LEFT"
		End if
		pe2.AddColumnDef Alanlar(i,0), Alanlar(i,3), Alanlar(i,4), Align
		pe2.SetRowValue_String -1, AlanAdi
	Next
	PE2.LineOut

	For satirNo = 1 to Tablo.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Excel 'e gönderiliyor : " & Tablo.GetAppName & " - " & i & " / " & Tablo.GetRowCount
		PE2.InitRowValues

		ExcelSatir = -1
		ncol = 0
		For i=0 To uBound(Alanlar,1)
			ncol = ncol+1
			if ncol > 256 Then Exit For
			AlanAdi = Alanlar(i,0)
			AlanTuru = Alanlar(i,2)
			If AlanTuru = "INT16" or AlanTuru = "INT32" or AlanTuru = "DOUBLE" Then
				Deger = Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo)
				pe2.SetRowValue_Double ExcelSatir, Deger
			ElseIf AlanTuru = "DATE" Then
				OutputStr=Doc.StrToNorDate(Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo))
				pe2.SetRowValue_String ExcelSatir,OutputStr
			Else
				pe2.SetRowValue_String ExcelSatir,Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo)
			End if
		Next
		PE2.LineOut
	Next
	pe2.Show
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Dim MTable
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "SIPNO", "CHAR",10,0,"R","F2=DO_BRW('STOK40E')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "CHSIPNO", "CHAR",20,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TOPLAM_SIP", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TOPLAM_SEC", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SEVKEMRIOPT", "CHAR",1,0,"R","",""
	MTable.SEVKEMRIOPT = "0"

	Dim MTableT
	Set MTableT=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET=STOK01(NOFILE)","IO")
	MTableT.Addfield2 "SELECTED", "CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Setd7FieldEvent "SELECTED","ONVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "SELECTED_OV"

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU1",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par
End Sub
Sub STOK01_Doldur()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")

	MTableT.SetRowAID 1,1,1

	SiparisNo = MTable.SIPNO
	if trim(SiparisNo) = "" Then
		Set S40E = Doc.Select1("fsf3f3f", "STOK40E", "CHSIPNO='" & MTable.CHSIPNO & "'", "", -1)
		if S40E.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			SiparisNo = S40E.EVRAKNO(1)
		End if
	End if
	if trim(SiparisNo) = "" Then Exit Sub

	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("dde34r55", "STOK01", "ALLOC_SIPNO='" & SiparisNo & "'&ALLOC_SYSSTATUS='2'", "", -1)
	Alanlar = MTableT.GetFieldNames("")
	MTableT.Empty()
	For i = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()		
		satirNo = MTableT.AddRow()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Rezervasyonlar Getiriliyor : " & i & " / " & S01.GetRowCount()
		MTableT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		For Each AlanAdi in Alanlar
			if AlanAdi <> "KOD_STOK00_AD" Then
				AlanDegeri = S01.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi, i)
				MTableT.SetFieldValue AlanAdi, -1, AlanDegeri
			End if
		Next
		MTableT.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", MTableT.KOD, "")
	Next
	MTableT.SetCurrentRow(1)
	MTableT.SetRowAID 0,0,0
	MTable.TOPLAM_SEC = MTableT.SF_MIKTAR
End Sub
Sub SevkEmriOlustur()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")

	Set MT = Doc.Select2("sdf5r55yy7", "MAKROTABLET", "SELECTED='E'", "", -1)
	if MT.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Satýr seçmediniz", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Set S30E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK30E")
	Set S30T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK30T")

	For i = 1 to MTableT.GetRowCount()
		if MTableT.SELECTED(i) = "E" Then
			Eklenen = Eklenen + 1
			if Eklenen = 1 and trim(MTable.SEVKEMRIOPT) = "0" Then
				Doc.New_Voucher
				S30E.AMBCODE = MTableT.AMBCODE(1)
				S30T.Empty
			End if
			Doc.StatusBarMessage "Aktarýlýyor : " & Eklenen
			satirNo = S30T.AddRow()
			S30T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			S30T.CH_KOD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40E", "MUSTERIKODU", MTableT.ALLOC_SIPNO(i), "")
			S30T.CH_KOD_CARI00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", S30T.CH_KOD, "")

			S30T.OALLOC_SYSSTATUS = MTableT.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_USTATUS = MTableT.ALLOC_USTATUS(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_EVRAKTYPE = MTableT.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_EVRAKNO = MTableT.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_SIPTYPE = MTableT.ALLOC_SIPTYPE(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_SIPNO = MTableT.ALLOC_SIPNO(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_SIPARTNO = MTableT.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_MPSEVNO = MTableT.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_MPSARTNO = MTableT.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_NONMOVABL = MTableT.ALLOC_NONMOVABL(i)

			Set S40T1 = Doc.Select1("d4546gg", "STOK40T", "ARTNO='" & MTableT.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i) & "'", "", -1)
			S30T.SP_REV_RTESTARIH = S40T1.REV_RTESTARIH
			S30T.SP_SF_PMIKTAR = S40T1.SF_PMIKTAR
			S30T.SP_SF_IMIKTAR = S40T1.SF_IMIKTAR
			S30T.KOD = MTableT.KOD(i)
			S30T.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", MTableT.KOD(i), "")
			S30T.LOTNUMBER = MTableT.LOTNUMBER(i)
			S30T.SERINO = MTableT.SERINO(i)
			S30T.SF_MIKTAR = MTableT.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			S30T.SF_SF_UNIT = MTableT.SF_UNIT(i)
			S30T.LOCATION1 = MTableT.LOCATION1(i)
			S30T.LOCATION2 = MTableT.LOCATION2(i)
			S30T.LOCATION3 = MTableT.LOCATION3(i)
			S30T.LOCATION4 = MTableT.LOCATION4(i)
			S30T.FOKOD = MTableT.FOKOD(i)
			S30T.SF_VRI_VR_C = MTableT.VRI_VR_C(i)
			S30T.SF_VRI_VR_R1 = MTableT.VRI_VR_R1(i)
			S30T.SF_VRI_VR_R2 = MTableT.VRI_VR_R2(i)
			S30T.SF_VRI_VR_R3 = MTableT.VRI_VR_R3(i)
			S30T.SF_VRI_NUM1 = MTableT.VRI_NUM1(i)
			S30T.SF_VRI_NUM2 = MTableT.VRI_NUM2(i)
			S30T.SF_AUTOCALC = "E"
			S30T.SF_STOK_MIKTAR	 = MTableT.STOK_MIKTAR(i)
			S30T.KOD_STOK00_IUNIT = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", MTableT.KOD(i), "")
			S30T.SP_TOLERANS = S40T1.TOLERANS
			S30T.SP_TOLERANSTYPE = S40T1.TOLERANSTYPE
		End if
	Next
	if Eklenen > 0 Then Doc.Save_Voucher
	Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL
	'Call STOK01_Doldur()
End Sub
Sub SELECTED_OV()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	if MTableT.SELECTED = "E" Then
		MTable.TOPLAM_SEC = MTable.TOPLAM_SEC + MTableT.SF_MIKTAR
	Else
		MTable.TOPLAM_SEC = MTable.TOPLAM_SEC - MTableT.SF_MIKTAR
	End if
End Sub
