Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : ER2011 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : SEVKEMRI2
Hangi Tablo için : STOK31
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Sevk Emri Oluþtur
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Dim MTable
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "SIPNO", "CHAR",10,0,"R","F2=DO_BRW('STOK40E')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "CHSIPNO", "CHAR",20,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TOPLAM_SIP", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TOPLAM_SEC", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SEVKEMRIOPT", "CHAR",1,0,"R","",""
	MTable.SEVKEMRIOPT = "0"

	Dim MTableT
	Set MTableT=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET=STOK01(NOFILE)","IO")
	MTableT.Addfield2 "SELECTED", "CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Setd7FieldEvent "SELECTED","ONVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "SELECTED_OV"

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU1",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par
End Sub
Sub STOK01_Doldur()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")

	MTableT.SetRowAID 1,1,1

	SiparisNo = MTable.SIPNO
	if trim(SiparisNo) = "" Then
		Set S40E = Doc.Select1("fsf3f3f", "STOK40E", "CHSIPNO='" & MTable.CHSIPNO & "'", "", -1)
		if S40E.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			SiparisNo = S40E.EVRAKNO(1)
		End if
	End if
	if trim(SiparisNo) = "" Then Exit Sub

	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("dde34r55", "STOK01", "ALLOC_SIPNO='" & SiparisNo & "'&ALLOC_SYSSTATUS='2'", "", -1)
	Alanlar = MTableT.GetFieldNames("")
	MTableT.Empty()
	For i = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()		
		satirNo = MTableT.AddRow()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Rezervasyonlar Getiriliyor : " & i & " / " & S01.GetRowCount()
		MTableT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		For Each AlanAdi in Alanlar
			if AlanAdi <> "KOD_STOK00_AD" Then
				AlanDegeri = S01.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi, i)
				MTableT.SetFieldValue AlanAdi, -1, AlanDegeri
			End if
		Next
		MTableT.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", MTableT.KOD, "")
	Next
	MTableT.SetCurrentRow(1)
	MTableT.SetRowAID 0,0,0
	MTable.TOPLAM_SEC = MTableT.SF_MIKTAR
End Sub
Sub SevkEmriOlustur()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")

	Set MT = Doc.Select2("sdf5r55yy7", "MAKROTABLET", "SELECTED='E'", "", -1)
	if MT.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Satýr seçmediniz", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Set S30E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK31E")
	Set S30T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK31T")

	For i = 1 to MTableT.GetRowCount()
		if MTableT.SELECTED(i) = "E" Then
			Eklenen = Eklenen + 1
			if Eklenen = 1 and trim(MTable.SEVKEMRIOPT) = "0" Then
				Doc.New_Voucher
				S30E.AMBCODE = MTableT.AMBCODE(1)
				S30E.TARGETAMBCODE = MTableT.TARGETAMBCODE(1)
				S30T.Empty
			End if
			Doc.StatusBarMessage "Aktarýlýyor : " & Eklenen
			satirNo = S30T.AddRow()
			S30T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
'			S30T.CH_KOD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40E", "MUSTERIKODU", MTableT.ALLOC_SIPNO(i), "")
'			S30T.CH_KOD_CARI00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", S30T.CH_KOD, "")

			S30T.OALLOC_SYSSTATUS = MTableT.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_USTATUS = MTableT.ALLOC_USTATUS(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_EVRAKTYPE = MTableT.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_EVRAKNO = MTableT.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_SIPTYPE = MTableT.ALLOC_SIPTYPE(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_SIPNO = MTableT.ALLOC_SIPNO(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_SIPARTNO = MTableT.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_MPSEVNO = MTableT.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_MPSARTNO = MTableT.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_NONMOVABL = MTableT.ALLOC_NONMOVABL(i)

			Set S40T1 = Doc.Select1("d4546gg", "STOK40T", "ARTNO='" & MTableT.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i) & "'", "", -1)
'			S30T.SP_REV_RTESTARIH = S40T1.REV_RTESTARIH
'			S30T.SP_SF_PMIKTAR = S40T1.SF_PMIKTAR
'			S30T.SP_SF_IMIKTAR = S40T1.SF_IMIKTAR
			S30T.KOD = MTableT.KOD(i)
			S30T.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", MTableT.KOD(i), "")
			S30T.LOTNUMBER = MTableT.LOTNUMBER(i)
			S30T.SERINO = MTableT.SERINO(i)
			S30T.SF_MIKTAR = MTableT.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			S30T.SF_SF_UNIT = MTableT.SF_UNIT(i)
			S30T.LOCATION1 = MTableT.LOCATION1(i)
			S30T.LOCATION2 = MTableT.LOCATION2(i)
			S30T.LOCATION3 = MTableT.LOCATION3(i)
			S30T.LOCATION4 = MTableT.LOCATION4(i)
			S30T.FOKOD = MTableT.FOKOD(i)
			S30T.SF_VRI_VR_C = MTableT.VRI_VR_C(i)
			S30T.SF_VRI_VR_R1 = MTableT.VRI_VR_R1(i)
			S30T.SF_VRI_VR_R2 = MTableT.VRI_VR_R2(i)
			S30T.SF_VRI_VR_R3 = MTableT.VRI_VR_R3(i)
			S30T.SF_VRI_NUM1 = MTableT.VRI_NUM1(i)
			S30T.SF_VRI_NUM2 = MTableT.VRI_NUM2(i)
			S30T.SF_AUTOCALC = "E"
			S30T.SF_STOK_MIKTAR	 = MTableT.STOK_MIKTAR(i)
			S30T.KOD_STOK00_IUNIT = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", MTableT.KOD(i), "")
'			S30T.SP_TOLERANS = S40T1.TOLERANS
'			S30T.SP_TOLERANSTYPE = S40T1.TOLERANSTYPE
		End if
	Next
	if Eklenen > 0 Then Doc.Save_Voucher
	Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL
	'Call STOK01_Doldur()
End Sub
Sub SELECTED_OV()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	if MTableT.SELECTED = "E" Then
		MTable.TOPLAM_SEC = MTable.TOPLAM_SEC + MTableT.SF_MIKTAR
	Else
		MTable.TOPLAM_SEC = MTable.TOPLAM_SEC - MTableT.SF_MIKTAR
	End if
End Sub

Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	for i = 1 to STOK24T.GetRecCount()
		if trim(STOK24T.KOD(i)) <> "" then
			Maliyet = Doc.SonHesaplanMaliyet(STOK24T.KOD(i),STOK24E.AMBCODE,STOK24E.TARIH)
			STOK24T.SetCurrentRow(i)
			STOK24T.OR_FIYAT(i) = Doc.Round(Maliyet,4)
			STOK24T.PUNIT_ORG(i) = "TL"
		end if
	Next
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	For i = 1 To STOK29T.GetRecCount()
		ToplamMasraf = ToplamMasraf + STOK29T.MASRAFPAYITUTAR(i)
		ToplamTLTutar = ToplamTLTutar + STOK29T.TL_TUTAR(i)
	Next
	ToplamTutar = ToplamTLTutar + ToplamMasraf

	Mesaj =		"Toplam Mal Bedeli : " & Doc.NumFormat(ToplamTLTutar,20,0) & " TL" & vbCrLf & _
					"Toplam Masraf       : " & Doc.NumFormat(ToplamMasraf,20,0) & " TL" & vbCrLf & _
					"Masraf Dahil Tutar : " & Doc.NumFormat(ToplamTutar,20,0) & " TL"
	k = Doc.d7Msgbox(Mesaj,"Dinamo",MB_OK)

End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : ER2011 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : TRACK262

Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : TRACK262 Ekran Formu
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Set STACK_PDA=Doc.GetTableObject("STACK_PDA")
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")

	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.CreateTableObject("STOK01_PDA=STOK01(NOFILE)","IO")
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION1","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION2","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION3","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION4","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SAYAC","DOUBLE",5,0,"R","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SF_PAKETICERIGI","DOUBLE",8,0,"R","",""

	Set STACK_PDA=Doc.CreateTableObject("STACK_PDA=STACK","IO")
	STACK_PDA.Addfield2 "PROPT","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	STACK_PDA.Addfield2 "AUTO_ADD","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""

	Set STOK26E=Doc.GetTableObject("STOK26E")

	MTable.Addfield2 "BARKOD","CHAR",30,0,"L","",""

	MTable.Setd7FieldEvent "BARKOD","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "BARKOD_AV"
	STOK01_PDA.Setd7FieldEvent "KOD","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "KOD_AV"

	STACK_PDA.PROPT = "1"
	STACK_PDA.AUTO_ADD = "0"
End Sub
Sub LoadSubForm2()
	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadMainForm_Dyn "USR_TRACK2612",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par	
End Sub
Sub BARKOD_AV()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")

	LocPrefix = trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("LOCPREFX"))
	LocSpace = trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("LOCSPACE"))

	if Left(MTable.BARKOD, 2) = LocPrefix Then
		Barkod = Mid(MTable.BARKOD, 3)
		BarkodArr = Split(Barkod, LocSpace)
		For i = 0 to uBound(BarkodArr)
			if i <= 3 Then
				STOK01_PDA.SetFieldValue "PRX_LOCATION" & i + 1, -1, BarkodArr(i)
			End if
		Next
	End if
End Sub
Sub KOD_AV()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set STOK26E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK26E")
	STOK01_PDA.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", STOK01_PDA.KOD, "")	

	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("sd24g24sdfhs", "STOK01", "KOD='" & STOK01_PDA.KOD & "'&AMBCODE='" & STOK26E.AMBCODE & "'&LOCATION1=' '", "", -1)
	Set S26T = Doc.Select1("rg4g4gdfhdh", "STOK26T", "EVRAKNO='" & STOK26E.EVRAKNO & "'&KOD='" & STOK01_PDA.KOD & "'&LOCATION1=' '", "", -1)
	Set S26T1 = Doc.Select2("ddertrg4g4gdfhdh", "STOK26T", "KOD='" & STOK01_PDA.KOD & "'&LOCATION1=' '", "", -1)
	Kalan = 0
	if S01.GetRowCount() > 0 Then Kalan = S01.SumOf("SF_MIKTAR")
	if S26T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then Kalan = Kalan + S26T.SumOf("SF_MIKTAR")
	if S26T1.GetRowCount() > 0 Then Kalan = Kalan - S26T1.SumOf("SF_MIKTAR")
	STOK01_PDA.SF_MIKTAR = Kalan
End Sub
Sub cmdEkle()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set STOK26T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK26T")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")

	STOK26T.SafePack()
	Set S01 = GetS01List()
	Kalan = STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI
	For i = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()
		if Kalan = 0 Then Exit For
		if S01.SF_MIKTAR(i) > 0 Then
			if Kalan <= S01.SF_MIKTAR(i) Then
				Yazilacak = Kalan
			Else
				Yazilacak = S01.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			End if
			Kalan = Kalan - Yazilacak
			satirNo = STOK26T.AddRow
			STOK26T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			STOK26T.KOD = STOK01_PDA.KOD
			Doc.CallFieldAfterValidate_Row "STOK26T", "KOD", STOK26T.GetCurrentRow

			STOK26T.SF_MIKTAR = Yazilacak
			Doc.CallFieldAfterValidate_Row "STOK26T", "SF_MIKTAR", STOK26T.GetCurrentRow

			STOK26T.LOCATION_NEW1 = STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION1
			STOK26T.LOCATION_NEW2 = STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION2
			STOK26T.LOCATION_NEW3 = STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION3
			STOK26T.LOCATION_NEW4 = STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION4

			STOK26T.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(satirNo) = S01.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(i)
			STOK26T.ALLOC_NONMOVABL(satirNo) = S01.ALLOC_NONMOVABL(i)
			STOK26T.ALLOC_SIPTYPE(satirNo) = S01.ALLOC_SIPTYPE(i)
			STOK26T.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(satirNo) = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(i)
			STOK26T.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(satirNo) = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(i)
			STOK26T.ALLOC_SIPNO(satirNo) = S01.ALLOC_SIPNO(i)
			STOK26T.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(satirNo) = S01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i)
			STOK26T.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(satirNo) = S01.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(i)
			STOK26T.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(satirNo) = S01.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(i)
		End if
	Next
	'STOK26T.D_ID = Doc.Sayac0danAl("SERINO")
	STOK01_PDA.Empty
	MTable.Empty
	STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = STOK26T.GetRowCount()
End Sub
Function GetS01List()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set STOK26E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK26E")
	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("sd24g24sdfhs", "STOK01", "KOD='" & STOK01_PDA.KOD & "'&AMBCODE='" & STOK26E.AMBCODE & "'&LOCATION1=' '", "", -1)
	Set S26T = Doc.Select1("rg4g4gdfhdh", "STOK26T", "EVRAKNO='" & STOK26E.EVRAKNO & "'&KOD='" & STOK01_PDA.KOD & "'&LOCATION_NEW1>' '", "", -1)
	Set S26T1 = Doc.Select2("ddertrg4g4gdfhdh", "STOK26T", "KOD='" & STOK01_PDA.KOD & "'&LOCATION_NEW1>' '", "", -1)

	For i = 1 to S26T.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = S01.AddRow()
		S01.KOD(satirNo) = S26T.KOD(i)
		S01.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = S26T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		S01.LOCATION1(satirNo) = S26T.LOCATION1(i)
		S01.LOCATION2(satirNo) = S26T.LOCATION2(i)
		S01.LOCATION3(satirNo) = S26T.LOCATION3(i)
		S01.LOCATION4(satirNo) = S26T.LOCATION4(i)
		S01.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(satirNo) = S26T.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(i)
		S01.ALLOC_NONMOVABL(satirNo) = S26T.ALLOC_NONMOVABL(i)
		S01.ALLOC_SIPTYPE(satirNo) = S26T.ALLOC_SIPTYPE(i)
		S01.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(satirNo) = S26T.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(i)
		S01.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(satirNo) = S26T.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(i)
		S01.ALLOC_SIPNO(satirNo) = S26T.ALLOC_SIPNO(i)
		S01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(satirNo) = S26T.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i)
		S01.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(satirNo) = S26T.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(i)
		S01.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(satirNo) = S26T.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(i)
	Next

	For i = 1 to S26T1.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = S01.AddRow()
		S01.KOD(satirNo) = S26T1.KOD(i)
		S01.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = S26T1.SF_MIKTAR(i) * -1
		S01.LOCATION1(satirNo) = S26T1.LOCATION1(i)
		S01.LOCATION2(satirNo) = S26T1.LOCATION2(i)
		S01.LOCATION3(satirNo) = S26T1.LOCATION3(i)
		S01.LOCATION4(satirNo) = S26T1.LOCATION4(i)
		S01.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(satirNo) = S26T1.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(i)
		S01.ALLOC_NONMOVABL(satirNo) = S26T1.ALLOC_NONMOVABL(i)
		S01.ALLOC_SIPTYPE(satirNo) = S26T1.ALLOC_SIPTYPE(i)
		S01.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(satirNo) = S26T1.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(i)
		S01.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(satirNo) = S26T1.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(i)
		S01.ALLOC_SIPNO(satirNo) = S26T1.ALLOC_SIPNO(i)
		S01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(satirNo) = S26T1.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i)
		S01.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(satirNo) = S26T1.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(i)
		S01.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(satirNo) = S26T1.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(i)
	Next
	S01.GroupBy("KOD+LOCATION1+LOCATION2+LOCATION3+LOCATION4+ALLOC_SYSSTATUS+ALLOC_NONMOVABL+ALLOC_SIPTYPE+ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE+ALLOC_EVRAKNO+ALLOC_SIPNO+ALLOC_SIPARTNO+ALLOC_SIPARTNO+ALLOC_MPSEVNO+ALLOC_MPSARTNO")
	Set GetS01List = S01
'	Call SendTableToExcel(S01)
End Function

Sub SendGridToExcel(Tablo, GridName)
	Set pe2 = Doc.CreateSObject("ExcelOutput")
	PE2.SetOrientation "P"
	PE2.PageB_ReportHead_std1 GridName

	Grid = Doc.GetGridColInfo("", GridName)
	ncol = 0
	For i=0 To uBound(Grid,1)
		if Grid(i,1) > 0 Then
			ncol = ncol+1
			if ncol > 256 Then Exit For
			AlanTuru = Grid(i,4)
			AlanAdi = Grid(i,0)
			AlanBasligi = Grid(i,2)

			If AlanTuru = "N" Then
				Align = "RIGHT"
			Else
				Align = "LEFT"
			End if
			pe2.AddColumnDef Grid(i,0), Grid(i,5), Grid(i,6), Align
			pe2.SetRowValue_String -1, AlanBasligi
		End if
	Next
	PE2.LineOut

	For satirNo = 1 to Tablo.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Excel 'e gönderiliyor : " & GridName & " - " & i & " / " & Tablo.GetRowCount
		PE2.InitRowValues

		ExcelSatir = -1
		ncol = 0
		For i=0 To uBound(Grid,1)
			if Grid(i,1) > 0 Then
				ncol = ncol+1
				if ncol > 256 Then Exit For
				AlanAdi = Grid(i,0)
				AlanTuru = Grid(i,4)
				If AlanTuru = "N" Then
					Deger = Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo)
					pe2.SetRowValue_Double ExcelSatir, Deger
				ElseIf AlanTuru = "D" Then
					OutputStr=Doc.StrToNorDate(Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo))
					pe2.SetRowValue_String ExcelSatir,OutputStr
				Else
					pe2.SetRowValue_String ExcelSatir,Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo)
				End if
			End if
		Next
		PE2.LineOut
	Next
	pe2.Show
End Sub

Sub SendTableToExcel(Tablo)
	Set pe2 = Doc.CreateSObject("ExcelOutput")
	PE2.SetOrientation "P"
	PE2.PageB_ReportHead_std1 Tablo.GetAppName

	Alanlar = Tablo.GetFields()
	ncol = 0
	For i=0 To uBound(Alanlar,1)
		AlanAdi = Alanlar(i,0)
		if Cikacak(AlanAdi) Then
			ncol = ncol+1
			if ncol > 256 Then Exit For
			AlanTuru = Alanlar(i,2)
				If AlanTuru = "INT16" or AlanTuru = "INT32" or AlanTuru = "DOUBLE" Then
				Align = "RIGHT"
			Else
				Align = "LEFT"
			End if
			pe2.AddColumnDef Alanlar(i,0), Alanlar(i,3), Alanlar(i,4), Align
			pe2.SetRowValue_String -1, AlanAdi
		End if
	Next
	PE2.LineOut

	For satirNo = 1 to Tablo.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Excel 'e gönderiliyor : " & Tablo.GetAppName & " - " & i & " / " & Tablo.GetRowCount
		PE2.InitRowValues

		ExcelSatir = -1
		ncol = 0
		For i=0 To uBound(Alanlar,1)
			AlanAdi = Alanlar(i,0)
			if Cikacak(AlanAdi) Then
				ncol = ncol+1
				if ncol > 256 Then Exit For
				AlanTuru = Alanlar(i,2)
				If AlanTuru = "INT16" or AlanTuru = "INT32" or AlanTuru = "DOUBLE" Then
					Deger = Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo)
					pe2.SetRowValue_Double ExcelSatir, Deger
				ElseIf AlanTuru = "DATE" Then
					OutputStr=Doc.StrToNorDate(Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo))
					pe2.SetRowValue_String ExcelSatir,OutputStr
				Else
					pe2.SetRowValue_String ExcelSatir,Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo)
				End if
			End if
		Next
		PE2.LineOut
	Next
	pe2.Show
End Sub
Function Cikacak(AlanAdi)
	Cikacak = True
	if InStr(1, AlanAdi, "BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE_") > 0 Then
		Cikacak = False
	End if
	if InStr(1, AlanAdi, "BOMREC_CODE_") > 0 Then
		Cikacak = False
	End if
End Function

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : ER2011 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : TRACK29
Hangi Tablo için : 
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : 
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	STOK01_PDA.AddField2 "BARKOD", "CHAR", 30, 0, "L", "", ""
	STOK01_PDA.Setd7FieldEvent "BARKOD","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "BARKOD_AV"

	Set STACK_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STACK_PDA")
	STACK_PDA.D7KIDSLB_STATU = "OK"
End Sub
Sub BARKOD_AV()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set STOK29E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK29E")

	Barkod = STOK01_PDA.BARKOD

	if LokasyonVeyaBarkod(Barkod) Then
		STOK01_PDA.D_ID2 = Barkod
	Else
		Call GetStokKoduveMiktar(Barkod, StokKodu, Miktar)
		if trim(StokKodu) = "" Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Geçersiz Barkod veya Stok Kodu", "", MB_OK
		Else
			STOK01_PDA.KOD = StokKodu
			STOK01_PDA.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", STOK01_PDA.KOD, "")
			STOK01_PDA.SF_MIKTAR = Miktar
			STOK01_PDA.SF_UNIT = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", STOK01_PDA.KOD, "")
		End if
	End if

	STOK01_PDA.BARKOD = ""
End Sub
Sub cmdEkle()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
'	Set S29T = Doc.Select2("asdf2fg24gsdg", "STOK29T", "SERINO='" & STOK01_PDA.SERINO & "'", "", -1)
'	if S29T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
'		Doc.d7MsgBox "Bu barkod daha önce okutulmuþ", "Ýþlem Ýptal" , MB_OK
'		Exit Sub
'	End if
	Set STOK29T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK29T")
	satirNo = STOK29T.AddRow()
	STOK29T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
	STOK29T.KOD = STOK01_PDA.KOD
	STOK29T.LOTNUMBER = STOK01_PDA.LOTNUMBER
	STOK29T.SERINO = STOK01_PDA.SERINO
	STOK29T.SF_MIKTAR = STOK01_PDA.SF_MIKTAR
	STOK29T.SetFieldValueNV "D_ID", -1, STOK01_PDA.SERINO

'	if trim(STOK01_PDA.D_ID) = "" then 	Doc.RunMacro0 "PFRM7S.ETK-HMD", "PrintLabel"

	STOK01_PDA.Empty
	STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = STOK29T.GetRowCount()
End Sub
Sub cmdKaydet()
	Set S29T = Doc.Select2("sdf2fgsdg", "STOK29T", "SIPARTNO<>'" & Space(16) & "'", "", -1)
	if S29T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then Doc.RunCommand "CMD_SPBAGLANTISI_IPTAL"

	Doc.RunCommand "CMD_SIRALI_SIPARIS_KAPATMA"
	if Doc.Save_Voucher = 0 Then
'		par1 = "ETK-HMD"
'		Doc.RunMacro1 "MAKR7S.ETIKET", "STOK29_EtiketYarat", par1
	End if
End Sub
Function LokasyonVeyaBarkod(ByVal Barkod)
	LokasyonVeyaBarkod = False
	LocPrefix = trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("LOCPREFX"))
	if LocPrefix <> "" Then
		if Left(Barkod, Len(LocPrefix)) = LocPrefix Then
			LokasyonVeyaBarkod = True
			Exit Function
		End if 
	End if

	Set D7LB = Doc.Select1("sef23ffdfsd", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Barkod & "'", "", -1)
	if D7LB.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		CarpimKatsayisi = 1
		Carpan = GetCarpan()
		if trim(Carpan) = "" Then
			LokasyonVeyaBarkod = False
			Exit Function
		Else
			CarpanPos = InStr(1, Barkod, trim(Carpan))
			if CarpanPos <= 1 Then
				LokasyonVeyaBarkod = False
				Exit Function
			Else
				CarpimKatsayisi = Doc.Cdbl(Left(Barkod, CarpanPos - 1))
				Barkod = Doc.LeftJustify(Mid(trim(Barkod), CarpanPos + 1), 30)
				Set D7LB = Doc.Select1("sef23ffdfsd", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Barkod & "'", "", -1)
				if D7LB.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
					LokasyonVeyaBarkod = False
					Exit Function
				End if
			End if
		End if
	End if
	LokasyonVeyaBarkod = True
End Function
Function GetStokKoduveMiktar(ByVal Barkod, StokKodu, Miktar)
	StokKodu = ""
	Miktar = 0
	Carpan = GetCarpan()
	if trim(Carpan) = "" Then
		StokKodu = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "KOD", Doc.LeftJustify(trim(Barkod), 24), "")
	Else
		CarpanPos = InStr(1, Barkod, trim(Carpan))
		if CarpanPos <= 1 Then
			StokKodu = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "KOD", Doc.LeftJustify(trim(Barkod), 24), "")
		Else
			Miktar = Doc.Cdbl(Left(Barkod, CarpanPos - 1))
			Barkod = Doc.LeftJustify(Mid(trim(Barkod), CarpanPos + 1), 30)
			StokKodu = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "KOD", Doc.LeftJustify(trim(Barkod), 24), "")
		End if
	End if
End Function
Function GetCarpan()
	GetCarpan = ""
	Set T75E = Doc.Select1("sdf24gdfgd", "TABL75E", "AKTIF_PASIF='*'", "", -1)
	if T75E.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		GetCarpan = T75E.MIKTAR_CARPAN_FLAG
	End if
End Function

Sub Makro1()
	Set Doc2 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")

	Set STACK_PDA=Doc.CreateTableObject("STACK_PDA=STACK","IO")
	STACK_PDA.Addfield2 "PROPT","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""

	Set STOK01_PDA=Doc.CreateTableObject("STOK01_PDA=STOK01(NOFILE)","IO")
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION1","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION2","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION3","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION4","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SF_PAKETICERIGI","DOUBLE",12,0,"R","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SF_MIKTAR_KALAN","DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SAYAC","DOUBLE",12,0,"R","",""

	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "BARKOD","CHAR",30,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "MPSNO","CHAR",10,0,"R","F2=DO_BRW('MMPS10E')=",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "URETIM_MIKTARI","DOUBLE",8,0,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TUMU","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "AMBCODE","CHAR",6,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('GDEF00')=",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TARGETAMBCODE","CHAR",6,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('GDEF00')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "CH_KODU","CHAR",16,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('CARI00')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "CH_KODU_CARI00_AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('CARI00','CH_KODU=KOD','AD', 'BRW_CARI00_AD')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "IRSALIYE_NO","CHAR",10,0,"R","DO_BRW('STOK60E','IRSALIYE_NO=EVRAKNO','EVRAKNO','BRW_STOK60E_1','MAKROTABLE_SF.IRSALIYE_NO',""EVRAKTYPE=='YC'"") ",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "STOK26_EVRAKNO","CHAR",10,0,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "URETIM_FASON","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""

	MTable.Setd7FieldEvent "BARKOD","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "BARKOD_AV"
	MTable.Setd7FieldEvent "CH_KODU","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "CH_KODU_AV"
	MTable.Setd7FieldEvent "MPSNO","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "MPSNO_AV"
	MTable.Setd7FieldEvent "URETIM_MIKTARI","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "URETIM_MIKTARI_AV"
	MTable.Setd7FieldEvent "TUMU","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "TUMU_AV"
	MTable.Setd7FieldEvent "IRSALIYE_NO","ONVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "IRSALIYE_NO_OV"


	Set MTableT = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("STOK01_PDA_LIST=STOK01(NOFILE)", "IO")
	MTableT.Addfield2 "OKUNDU10","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "LOKASYON","CHAR",11,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "OKUTULAN_LOKASYON","CHAR",11,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "OKUTULAN_MIKTAR","DOUBLE",11,0,"R","",""

End Sub
Sub UretimeCikisMain()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	MTable.URETIM_FASON = "U"
	Call VarsayilanDepolar()

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK26", "")

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadMainForm_Dyn "TRACK32_2X1",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par	
End Sub

	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	MTable.URETIM_FASON = "F"
	Call VarsayilanDepolar()
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK63", "")

	Doc.LoadMainForm_Dyn "TRACK32_2X1F",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par	
End Sub
Sub MalzemeToplaMain()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	if MTable.URETIM_FASON = "F" Then
		if trim(MTable.IRSALIYE_NO) = "" Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Ýrsaliye No Boþ", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
			Exit Sub
		End if
		if trim(MTable.CH_KODU) = "" Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Fasoncu girilmemiþ", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
			Exit Sub
		End if
	End if
	if trim(MTable.AMBCODE) = "" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Veren Depo girilmemiþ", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	if trim(MTable.TARGETAMBCODE) = "" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Alan Depo girilmemiþ", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadMainForm_Dyn "TRACK32_2X2",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par	
End Sub
Sub MPSNO_AV()
	Call TransferEdilecekMiktarlariHesapla()
End Sub
Sub TUMU_AV()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	MTable.URETIM_MIKTARI = 0
	Call TransferEdilecekMiktarlariHesapla()
End Sub
Sub URETIM_MIKTARI_AV()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	MTable.TUMU = "0"
	Call TransferEdilecekMiktarlariHesapla()
End Sub
Sub CH_KODU_AV()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	MTable.CH_KODU_CARI00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", MTable.CH_KODU, "")
End Sub
Sub IRSALIYE_NO_OV()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	if trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK60E", "EVRAKNO", MTable.IRSALIYE_NO, "")) <> "" Then
		Doc.d7msgBox "Bu irsaliye daha önce kaydedilmiþ", "Ýþlem Kesildi", "MB_OK"
		MTable.IRSALIYE_NO = ""
	End if
End Sub
Sub TransferEdilecekMiktarlariHesapla()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA_LIST")
	MTableT.Empty

	if trim(MTable.MPSNO) = "" or (MTable.URETIM_MIKTARI = 0 and MTable.TUMU = "0") Then Exit Sub

	if trim(MTable.AMBCODE) = "" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Veren Depo ve Alan Depo doldurulmalýdýr", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("sdf234gdfh", "STOK01", "AMBCODE='" & MTable.AMBCODE & "'&ALLOC_MPSEVNO='" & MTable.MPSNO & "'", "LOCATION1+LOCATION2+LOCATION3", -1)
	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then 
		Doc.d7MsgBox trim(MTable.AMBCODE) & " deposunda " & trim(MTable.MPSNO) & " no.lu MPS için rezerve edilmiþ malzeme yok", "", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	if MTable.TUMU = "1" Then
		Call MPSIcinTransferEdilenTumHammaddeyiGetir(MTableT, S01)
	Else
		Call UretilecekMiktarKadarHesapla(MTableT, S01)
	End if
End Sub
Sub UretilecekMiktarKadarHesapla(MTableT, S01_Toplam)
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")

	MPSMiktari = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10E", "SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR", MTable.MPSNO, "")
	Set M10T = Doc.Select1("sdf3dfh", "MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO='" & MTable.MPSNO & "'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='H'", "", -1)

	MTableT.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	For i = 1 to M10T.GetRowCount()
		TransferEdilecekMiktar = Doc.Div(MTable.URETIM_MIKTARI * M10T.R_MIKTAR0(i), MPSMiktari)
		Kalan = TransferEdilecekMiktar
		Set S01 = Doc.Select2("sdf34hdfh", S01_Toplam.GetAppName, "KOD='" & M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i) & "'", "", -1)
		For j = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()
			if Kalan = 0 Then Exit For
			if Kalan <= S01.SF_MIKTAR(j) Then
				TrMiktar = Kalan
			Else
				TrMiktar = S01.SF_MIKTAR(j)
			End if
			Kalan = Kalan - TrMiktar

			satirNo = MTableT.AddRow()
			MTableT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			MTableT.KOD = S01.KOD(j)
			MTableT.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", S01.KOD(j), "")
			MTableT.LOTNUMBER = S01.LOTNUMBER(j)
			MTableT.SERINO = S01.SERINO(j)
			MTableT.SF_MIKTAR = TrMiktar
			MTableT.SF_UNIT = S01.SF_UNIT(j)
			MTableT.AMBCODE = S01.AMBCODE(j)
			MTableT.LOCATION1 = S01.LOCATION1(j)
			MTableT.LOCATION2 = S01.LOCATION2(j)
			MTableT.LOCATION3 = S01.LOCATION3(j)
			MTableT.LOCATION4 = S01.LOCATION4(j)
			MTableT.LOKASYON = trim(S01.LOCATION1(j)) & "-" & trim(S01.LOCATION2(j)) & "-" & trim(S01.LOCATION3(j)) ' & "-" & trim(S01.LOCATION4(j))
			MTableT.FOKOD = S01.FOKOD(j)
			MTableT.VRI_VR_R1 = S01.VRI_VR_R1(j)
			MTableT.VRI_VR_R2 = S01.VRI_VR_R2(j)
			MTableT.VRI_VR_R3 = S01.VRI_VR_R3(j)
			MTableT.VRI_NUM1 = S01.VRI_NUM1(j)
			MTableT.VRI_NUM2 = S01.VRI_NUM2(j)
			MTableT.VRI_VR_C = S01.VRI_VR_C(j)
			MTableT.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS = S01.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(j)
			MTableT.ALLOC_NONMOVABL = S01.ALLOC_NONMOVABL(j)
			MTableT.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(j)
			MTableT.ALLOC_EVRAKNO = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(j)
			MTableT.ALLOC_SIPTYPE = S01.ALLOC_SIPTYPE(j)
			MTableT.ALLOC_SIPNO = S01.ALLOC_SIPNO(j)
			MTableT.ALLOC_SIPARTNO = S01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(j)
			MTableT.ALLOC_MPSEVNO = S01.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(j)
			MTableT.ALLOC_MPSARTNO = S01.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(j)
			MTableT.OKUTULAN_LOKASYON = "---"
		Next
	Next
	MTableT.SetCurrentRow(1)
	MTableT.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub
Sub MPSIcinTransferEdilenTumHammaddeyiGetir(MTableT, S01)
	MTableT.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	For i = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MTableT.AddRow()
		MTableT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		MTableT.KOD = S01.KOD(i)
		MTableT.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", S01.KOD(i), "")
		MTableT.LOTNUMBER = S01.LOTNUMBER(i)
		MTableT.SERINO = S01.SERINO(i)
		MTableT.SF_MIKTAR = S01.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		MTableT.SF_UNIT = S01.SF_UNIT(i)
		MTableT.AMBCODE = S01.AMBCODE(i)
		MTableT.LOCATION1 = S01.LOCATION1(i)
		MTableT.LOCATION2 = S01.LOCATION2(i)
		MTableT.LOCATION3 = S01.LOCATION3(i)
		MTableT.LOCATION4 = S01.LOCATION4(i)
		MTableT.LOKASYON = trim(S01.LOCATION1(i)) & "-" & trim(S01.LOCATION2(i)) & "-" & trim(S01.LOCATION3(i)) ' & "-" & trim(S01.LOCATION4(i))
		MTableT.FOKOD = S01.FOKOD(i)
		MTableT.VRI_VR_R1 = S01.VRI_VR_R1(i)
		MTableT.VRI_VR_R2 = S01.VRI_VR_R2(i)
		MTableT.VRI_VR_R3 = S01.VRI_VR_R3(i)
		MTableT.VRI_NUM1 = S01.VRI_NUM1(i)
		MTableT.VRI_NUM2 = S01.VRI_NUM2(i)
		MTableT.VRI_VR_C = S01.VRI_VR_C(i)
		MTableT.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS = S01.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(i)
		MTableT.ALLOC_NONMOVABL = S01.ALLOC_NONMOVABL(i)
		MTableT.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(i)
		MTableT.ALLOC_EVRAKNO = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(i)
		MTableT.ALLOC_SIPTYPE = S01.ALLOC_SIPTYPE(i)
		MTableT.ALLOC_SIPNO = S01.ALLOC_SIPNO(i)
		MTableT.ALLOC_SIPARTNO = S01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i)
		MTableT.ALLOC_MPSEVNO = S01.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(i)
		MTableT.ALLOC_MPSARTNO = S01.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(i)
		MTableT.OKUTULAN_LOKASYON = "---"
	Next
	MTableT.SetCurrentRow(1)
	MTableT.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub
Sub BARKOD_AV()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set STACK_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STACK_PDA")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK")

	Barkod = MTable.BARKOD

	LocPrefix = trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("LOCPREFX"))
	if Left(MTable.BARKOD, Len(LocPrefix)) = LocPrefix Then
		Call LokasyonBilgileriniDodur()
	Else
		Call MiktarBilgisiniDoldur()
	End if
End Sub
Sub MiktariSifirla()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI = 0
End Sub
Sub MiktarBilgisiniDoldur()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA_LIST")

	Barkod = 	MTable.BARKOD
	MTable.BARKOD = ""

	CarpimKatsayisi = 1
	Set D7LB = Doc.Select1("sef23ffdfsd", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Barkod & "'", "", -1)
	if D7LB.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Set T75E = Doc.Select1("sdf24gdfgd", "TABL75E", "AKTIF_PASIF='*'", "", -1)
		if T75E.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			Carpan = T75E.MIKTAR_CARPAN_FLAG
			if trim(Carpan) = "" Then
				Doc.d7MsgBox "Geçersiz Barkod", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
				Exit Sub
			Else
				CarpanPos = InStr(1, Barkod, trim(Carpan))
				if CarpanPos <= 1 Then
					Doc.d7MsgBox "Geçersiz Barkod", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
					Exit Sub
				Else
					CarpimKatsayisi = Doc.Cdbl(Left(Barkod, CarpanPos - 1))
					Barkod = Doc.LeftJustify(Mid(trim(Barkod), CarpanPos + 1), 30)
					Set D7LB = Doc.Select1("sef23ffdfsd", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Barkod & "'", "", -1)
					if D7LB.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
						Doc.d7MsgBox "Geçersiz Barkod", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
						Exit Sub
					End if
				End if
			End if
		Else
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Geçersiz Barkod", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
			Exit Sub
		End if
	End if

	if trim(D7LB.SKOD) <> trim(MTableT.KOD) Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Okutulan Barkoda ait stok kodu : " & trim(D7LB.SKOD) & vbcrlf & "Seçilen satýrdaki stok kodu : " & trim(MTableT.KOD) & vbcrlf & "Farklý", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	MTableT.OKUTULAN_MIKTAR = CarpimKatsayisi * D7LB.SF_MIKTAR
	if MTableT.OKUTULAN_LOKASYON = MTableT.LOKASYON and Doc.Ceil(MTableT.SF_MIKTAR) = MTableT.OKUTULAN_MIKTAR Then
		MTableT.OKUNDU10 = "1"
	Else
		MTableT.OKUNDU10 = "0"
	End if
End Sub
Sub LokasyonBilgileriniDodur()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA_LIST")

	Barkod = 	MTable.BARKOD
	MTable.BARKOD = ""

	LocPrefix = trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("LOCPREFX"))
	Lokasyon = Mid(Barkod, Len(LocPrefix) + 1)

	if trim(Lokasyon) = "" Then Exit Sub

	LocSpace = trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("LOCSPACE"))
	if LocSpace = "" Then Exit Sub

	LokArray = Split(Lokasyon, LocSpace)
'	if uBound(LokArray) < 1 Then Exit Sub

	Lokasyon = ""
	For each Lok in LokArray
		Lokasyon = Lokasyon & trim(Lok) & "-"
	Next
	if trim(Lokasyon) <> "" Then Lokasyon = Left(Lokasyon, Len(Lokasyon) - 1)
	MTableT.OKUTULAN_LOKASYON = Lokasyon
	if MTableT.OKUTULAN_LOKASYON = MTableT.LOKASYON and MTableT.SF_MIKTAR = MTableT.OKUTULAN_MIKTAR Then
		MTableT.OKUNDU10 = "1"
	Else
		MTableT.OKUNDU10 = "0"
	End if
End Sub
Sub EkraniTemizle()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	
	MTable.Empty
	STOK01_PDA.Empty
	MTableT.Empty
End Sub
Sub SonIslemIptal()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")

	if trim(MTable.STOK26_EVRAKNO) = "" Then Exit Sub

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK26", "")
	Set STOK26T = Doc1.GettableObject("STOK26T")
	Doc1.Load_Voucher MTable.STOK26_EVRAKNO
	Doc1.Delete_Vouhcer
End Sub
Sub TransferFisiOlustur()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	if MTable.URETIM_FASON = "U" Then
		Call DepodanDepoyaTransferFisiOlustur()
	Else
		if TumOkutmalarTamamlanmamis() Then Exit Sub

		Call FasonaCikisIrsaliyesiOlustur()
	End if
End Sub
Sub DepodanDepoyaTransferFisiOlustur()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA_LIST")

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK26", "")
	Set STOK26E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK26E")
	Set STOK26T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK26T")

	Doc1.New_Voucher
	STOK26E.AMBCODE = MTable.AMBCODE
	STOK26E.TARGETAMBCODE = MTable.TARGETAMBCODE

	For i = 1 to MTableT.GetRowCount()
'MSGBOX MTableT.OKUNDU10(i)
		if MTableT.OKUNDU10(i) = "1" then
			satirNo = STOK26T.AddRow()
			STOK26T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

			STOK26T.KOD = MTableT.KOD(i)
			STOK26T.LOTNUMBER = MTableT.LOTNUMBER(i)
			STOK26T.SERINO = MTableT.SERINO(i)
			STOK26T.SF_MIKTAR = MTableT.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			STOK26T.SF_SF_UNIT = MTableT.SF_UNIT(i)
			STOK26T.AMBCODE = MTableT.AMBCODE(i)
			STOK26T.LOCATION1 = MTableT.LOCATION1(i)
			STOK26T.LOCATION2 = MTableT.LOCATION2(i)
			STOK26T.LOCATION3 = MTableT.LOCATION3(i)
			STOK26T.LOCATION4 = MTableT.LOCATION4(i)
			STOK26T.FOKOD = MTableT.FOKOD(i)
			STOK26T.SF_VRI_VR_R1 = MTableT.VRI_VR_R1(i)
			STOK26T.SF_VRI_VR_R2 = MTableT.VRI_VR_R2(i)
			STOK26T.SF_VRI_VR_R3 = MTableT.VRI_VR_R3(i)
			STOK26T.SF_VRI_NUM1 = MTableT.VRI_NUM1(i)
			STOK26T.SF_VRI_NUM2 = MTableT.VRI_NUM2(i)
			STOK26T.SF_VRI_VR_C = MTableT.VRI_VR_C(i)
			STOK26T.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS = MTableT.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(i)
			STOK26T.ALLOC_NONMOVABL = MTableT.ALLOC_NONMOVABL(i)
			STOK26T.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE = MTableT.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(i)
			STOK26T.ALLOC_EVRAKNO = MTableT.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(i)
			STOK26T.ALLOC_SIPTYPE = MTableT.ALLOC_SIPTYPE(i)
			STOK26T.ALLOC_SIPNO = MTableT.ALLOC_SIPNO(i)
			STOK26T.ALLOC_SIPARTNO = MTableT.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i)
			STOK26T.ALLOC_MPSEVNO = MTableT.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(i)
			STOK26T.ALLOC_MPSARTNO = MTableT.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(i)
		End if
	Next
	ret = Doc1.Save_Voucher
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher

	if ret = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Ýþlem tamamlandý", "", MB_OK
		MTable.Empty
		MTableT.Empty
		Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL
		MTable.URETIM_FASON = "U"
		Call VarsayilanDepolar()
	End if
End Sub
Sub FasonaCikisIrsaliyesiOlustur()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA_LIST")

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK63", "")
	Set STOK60E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK60E")
	Set STOK63T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK63T")

	ret = Doc1.Load_Voucher(MTable.IRSALIYE_NO)
	if ret <> 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Ýrsaliye yüklenemedi", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	STOK60E.CH_KODU = MTable.CH_KODU
	STOK60E.AMBCODE = MTable.AMBCODE
	STOK60E.TARGETAMBCODE = MTable.TARGETAMBCODE

	For i = 1 to MTableT.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = STOK63T.AddRow()
		STOK63T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

		STOK63T.KOD = MTableT.KOD(i)
		STOK63T.LOTNUMBER = MTableT.LOTNUMBER(i)
		STOK63T.SERINO = MTableT.SERINO(i)
		STOK63T.SF_MIKTAR = MTableT.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		STOK63T.SF_SF_UNIT = MTableT.SF_UNIT(i)
		STOK63T.AMBCODE = MTableT.AMBCODE(i)
		STOK63T.LOCATION1 = MTableT.LOCATION1(i)
		STOK63T.LOCATION2 = MTableT.LOCATION2(i)
		STOK63T.LOCATION3 = MTableT.LOCATION3(i)
		STOK63T.LOCATION4 = MTableT.LOCATION4(i)
		STOK63T.FOKOD = MTableT.FOKOD(i)
		STOK63T.SF_VRI_VR_R1 = MTableT.VRI_VR_R1(i)
		STOK63T.SF_VRI_VR_R2 = MTableT.VRI_VR_R2(i)
		STOK63T.SF_VRI_VR_R3 = MTableT.VRI_VR_R3(i)
		STOK63T.SF_VRI_NUM1 = MTableT.VRI_NUM1(i)
		STOK63T.SF_VRI_NUM2 = MTableT.VRI_NUM2(i)
		STOK63T.SF_VRI_VR_C = MTableT.VRI_VR_C(i)
		STOK63T.OALLOC_SYSSTATUS = MTableT.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(i)
		STOK63T.OALLOC_NONMOVABL = MTableT.ALLOC_NONMOVABL(i)
		STOK63T.OALLOC_EVRAKTYPE = MTableT.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(i)
		STOK63T.OALLOC_EVRAKNO = MTableT.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(i)
		STOK63T.OALLOC_SIPTYPE = MTableT.ALLOC_SIPTYPE(i)
		STOK63T.OALLOC_SIPNO = MTableT.ALLOC_SIPNO(i)
		STOK63T.OALLOC_SIPARTNO = MTableT.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i)
		STOK63T.OALLOC_MPSEVNO = MTableT.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(i)
		STOK63T.OALLOC_MPSARTNO = MTableT.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(i)

		STOK63T.NALLOC_SYSSTATUS = MTableT.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(i)
		STOK63T.NALLOC_NONMOVABL = MTableT.ALLOC_NONMOVABL(i)
		STOK63T.NALLOC_EVRAKTYPE = MTableT.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(i)
		STOK63T.NALLOC_EVRAKNO = MTableT.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(i)
		STOK63T.NALLOC_SIPTYPE = MTableT.ALLOC_SIPTYPE(i)
		STOK63T.NALLOC_SIPNO = MTableT.ALLOC_SIPNO(i)
		STOK63T.NALLOC_SIPARTNO = MTableT.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i)
		STOK63T.NALLOC_MPSEVNO = MTableT.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(i)
		STOK63T.NALLOC_MPSARTNO = MTableT.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(i)
	Next
	ret = Doc1.Save_Voucher
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher

	if ret = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Ýþlem tamamlandý", "", MB_OK
		MTable.Empty
		MTableT.Empty
		Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL
		MTable.URETIM_FASON = "F"
		Call VarsayilanDepolar()
	End if
End Sub
Sub VarsayilanDepolar()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	MTable.AMBCODE = "HK"
	if MTable.URETIM_FASON = "U" Then
		MTable.TARGETAMBCODE = "HIM"
	Else
		MTable.TARGETAMBCODE = ""
	End if
End Sub
Function TumOkutmalarTamamlanmamis()
	TumOkutmalarTamamlanmamis = False

	Set S01L = Doc.Select2("sdf34grhdh", "STOK01_PDA_LIST", "OKUNDU10<>'1'", "", -1)
	if S01L.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Tüm satýrlar okutulmamýþ", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		TumOkutmalarTamamlanmamis = True
	End if
End Function

Sub Makro1()
	Set Doc2 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")

	Set STACK_PDA=Doc.CreateTableObject("STACK_PDA=STACK","IO")
	STACK_PDA.Addfield2 "PROPT","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""

	Set STOK01_PDA=Doc.CreateTableObject("STOK01_PDA=STOK01(NOFILE)","IO")
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION1","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION2","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION3","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION4","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SF_PAKETICERIGI","DOUBLE",12,0,"R","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SF_MIKTAR_KALAN","DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SAYAC","DOUBLE",12,0,"R","",""

	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "BARKOD","CHAR",30,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SEVK_EMRI_NO","CHAR",10,0,"R","F2=DO_BRW('MMPS10E')=",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "AMBCODE","CHAR",6,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('GDEF00')=",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TARGETAMBCODE","CHAR",6,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('GDEF00')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "CH_KODU","CHAR",16,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('CARI00')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "CH_KODU_CARI00_AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('CARI00','CH_KODU=KOD','AD', 'BRW_CARI00_AD')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "IRSALIYE_NO","CHAR",10,0,"R","DO_BRW('STOK60E','IRSALIYE_NO=EVRAKNO','EVRAKNO','BRW_STOK60E_1','MAKROTABLE_SF.IRSALIYE_NO',""EVRAKTYPE=='YC'"") ",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "STOK60_EVRAKNO","CHAR",10,0,"R","",""

	MTable.Setd7FieldEvent "BARKOD","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "BARKOD_AV"
	MTable.Setd7FieldEvent "CH_KODU","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "CH_KODU_AV"
	MTable.Setd7FieldEvent "SEVK_EMRI_NO","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "SEVK_EMRI_NO_AV"
	MTable.Setd7FieldEvent "URETIM_MIKTARI","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "URETIM_MIKTARI_AV"
	MTable.Setd7FieldEvent "TUMU","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "TUMU_AV"
	MTable.Setd7FieldEvent "IRSALIYE_NO","ONVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "IRSALIYE_NO_OV"

	Set MTableT = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("STOK01_PDA_LIST=STOK01(NOFILE)", "IO")
	MTableT.Addfield2 "OKUNDU10","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "LOKASYON","CHAR",11,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "OKUTULAN_LOKASYON","CHAR",11,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "OKUTULAN_MIKTAR","DOUBLE",11,0,"R","",""

	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
'	Call VarsayilanDepolar()
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK61", "")
End Sub
Sub MalzemeToplaMain()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	if trim(MTable.IRSALIYE_NO) = "" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Ýrsaliye No Boþ", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	if trim(MTable.CH_KODU) = "" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Fasoncu girilmemiþ", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	if trim(MTable.AMBCODE) = "" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Veren Depo girilmemiþ", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	if trim(MTable.TARGETAMBCODE) = "" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Alan Depo girilmemiþ", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadMainForm_Dyn "TRACK33_2X2",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par	
End Sub
Sub SEVK_EMRI_NO_AV()
	Call TransferEdilecekMiktarlariHesapla()
End Sub
Sub CH_KODU_AV()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	MTable.CH_KODU_CARI00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", MTable.CH_KODU, "")
End Sub
Sub IRSALIYE_NO_OV()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	if trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK60E", "EVRAKNO", MTable.IRSALIYE_NO, "")) <> "" Then
		Doc.d7msgBox "Bu irsaliye daha önce kaydedilmiþ", "Ýþlem Kesildi", "MB_OK"
		MTable.IRSALIYE_NO = ""
	End if
End Sub
Sub TransferEdilecekMiktarlariHesapla()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA_LIST")

	MTableT.Empty

	if trim(MTable.SEVK_EMRI_NO) = "" Then Exit Sub
	MTable.AMBCODE = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK31E", "AMBCODE", MTable.SEVK_EMRI_NO, "")
	MTable.TARGETAMBCODE = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK31E", "TARGETAMBCODE", MTable.SEVK_EMRI_NO, "")

	if trim(MTable.TARGETAMBCODE) = "KL" then
		MTable.CH_KODU = "ML0001"
		MTable.CH_KODU_CARI00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", Doc.LeftJustify(trim(MTable.CH_KODU),16), "")
	Elseif trim(MTable.TARGETAMBCODE) = "IK" then
		MTable.CH_KODU = "MI0038"
		MTable.CH_KODU_CARI00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", Doc.LeftJustify(trim(MTable.CH_KODU),16), "")
	Elseif trim(MTable.TARGETAMBCODE) = "EA" then
		MTable.CH_KODU = "ME0007"
		MTable.CH_KODU_CARI00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", Doc.LeftJustify(trim(MTable.CH_KODU),16), "")
	End if

	irsaliyeno = doc.GetUDFValue("STOK31E",trim(MTable.SEVK_EMRI_NO),"IRSALIYENO",0)

	MTable.IRSALIYE_NO = Doc.LeftJustify(trim(irsaliyeno),6)


'	if trim(MTable.AMBCODE) = "" Then
'		Doc.d7MsgBox "Veren Depo ve Alan Depo doldurulmalýdýr", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
'		Exit Sub
'	End if

	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("sdf234gdfh", "STOK01", "AMBCODE='" & MTable.AMBCODE & "'&ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE='IV'&ALLOC_EVRAKNO='" & MTable.SEVK_EMRI_NO & "'", "LOCATION1+LOCATION2+LOCATION3", -1)
	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then 
		Doc.d7MsgBox trim(MTable.AMBCODE) & " deposunda " & trim(MTable.SEVK_EMRI_NO) & " no.lu depolara arsý sevk emri ile rezerve edilmiþ malzeme yok", "", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Call TransferEdilecekleriGetir(MTableT, S01)
End Sub
Sub TransferEdilecekleriGetir(MTableT, S01)
	MTableT.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	For i = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MTableT.AddRow()
		MTableT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		MTableT.KOD = S01.KOD(i)
		MTableT.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", S01.KOD(i), "")
		MTableT.LOTNUMBER = S01.LOTNUMBER(i)
		MTableT.SERINO = S01.SERINO(i)
		MTableT.SF_MIKTAR = S01.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		MTableT.SF_UNIT = S01.SF_UNIT(i)
		MTableT.AMBCODE = S01.AMBCODE(i)
		MTableT.LOCATION1 = S01.LOCATION1(i)
		MTableT.LOCATION2 = S01.LOCATION2(i)
		MTableT.LOCATION3 = S01.LOCATION3(i)
		MTableT.LOCATION4 = S01.LOCATION4(i)
		MTableT.LOKASYON = trim(S01.LOCATION1(i)) & "-" & trim(S01.LOCATION2(i)) & "-" & trim(S01.LOCATION3(i)) & "-" & trim(S01.LOCATION4(i))
		MTableT.FOKOD = S01.FOKOD(i)
		MTableT.VRI_VR_R1 = S01.VRI_VR_R1(i)
		MTableT.VRI_VR_R2 = S01.VRI_VR_R2(i)
		MTableT.VRI_VR_R3 = S01.VRI_VR_R3(i)
		MTableT.VRI_NUM1 = S01.VRI_NUM1(i)
		MTableT.VRI_NUM2 = S01.VRI_NUM2(i)
		MTableT.VRI_VR_C = S01.VRI_VR_C(i)
		MTableT.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS = S01.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(i)
		MTableT.ALLOC_NONMOVABL = S01.ALLOC_NONMOVABL(i)
		MTableT.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(i)
		MTableT.ALLOC_EVRAKNO = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(i)
		MTableT.ALLOC_SIPTYPE = S01.ALLOC_SIPTYPE(i)
		MTableT.ALLOC_SIPNO = S01.ALLOC_SIPNO(i)
		MTableT.ALLOC_SIPARTNO = S01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i)
		MTableT.ALLOC_MPSEVNO = S01.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(i)
		MTableT.ALLOC_MPSARTNO = S01.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(i)
		MTableT.OKUTULAN_LOKASYON = "---"
	Next
	MTableT.SetCurrentRow(1)
	MTableT.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub
Sub BARKOD_AV()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set STACK_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STACK_PDA")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK")

	Barkod = MTable.BARKOD

	LocPrefix = trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("LOCPREFX"))
	if Left(MTable.BARKOD, Len(LocPrefix)) = LocPrefix Then
		Call LokasyonBilgileriniDodur()
	Else
		Call MiktarBilgisiniDoldur()
	End if
End Sub
Sub MiktariSifirla()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI = 0
End Sub
Sub MiktarBilgisiniDoldur()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA_LIST")

	Barkod = 	MTable.BARKOD
	MTable.BARKOD = ""

	CarpimKatsayisi = 1
	Set D7LB = Doc.Select1("sef23ffdfsd", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Barkod & "'", "", -1)
	if D7LB.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Set T75E = Doc.Select1("sdf24gdfgd", "TABL75E", "AKTIF_PASIF='*'", "", -1)
		if T75E.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			Carpan = T75E.MIKTAR_CARPAN_FLAG
			if trim(Carpan) = "" Then
				Doc.d7MsgBox "Geçersiz Barkod", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
				Exit Sub
			Else
				CarpanPos = InStr(1, Barkod, trim(Carpan))
				if CarpanPos <= 1 Then
					Doc.d7MsgBox "Geçersiz Barkod", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
					Exit Sub
				Else
					CarpimKatsayisi = Doc.Cdbl(Left(Barkod, CarpanPos - 1))
					Barkod = Doc.LeftJustify(Mid(trim(Barkod), CarpanPos + 1), 30)
					Set D7LB = Doc.Select1("sef23ffdfsd", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Barkod & "'", "", -1)
					if D7LB.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
						Doc.d7MsgBox "Geçersiz Barkod", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
						Exit Sub
					End if
				End if
			End if
		Else
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Geçersiz Barkod", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
			Exit Sub
		End if
	End if

	if trim(D7LB.SKOD) <> trim(MTableT.KOD) Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Okutulan Barkoda ait stok kodu : " & trim(D7LB.SKOD) & vbcrlf & "Seçilen satýrdaki stok kodu : " & trim(MTableT.KOD) & vbcrlf & "Farklý", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	MTableT.OKUTULAN_MIKTAR = CarpimKatsayisi * D7LB.SF_MIKTAR
	if MTableT.OKUTULAN_LOKASYON = MTableT.LOKASYON and MTableT.SF_MIKTAR = MTableT.OKUTULAN_MIKTAR Then
		MTableT.OKUNDU10 = "1"
	Else
		MTableT.OKUNDU10 = "0"
	End if
End Sub
Sub LokasyonBilgileriniDodur()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA_LIST")

	Barkod = 	MTable.BARKOD
	MTable.BARKOD = ""




	LocPrefix = trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("LOCPREFX"))
	Lokasyon = Mid(Barkod, Len(LocPrefix) + 1)

	if trim(Lokasyon) = "" Then Exit Sub

	LocSpace = trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("LOCSPACE"))
	if LocSpace = "" Then Exit Sub

	LokArray = Split(Lokasyon, LocSpace)
'	if uBound(LokArray) < 1 Then Exit Sub

	Lokasyon = ""
	For each Lok in LokArray
		Lokasyon = Lokasyon & trim(Lok) & "-"
	Next
	if trim(Lokasyon) <> "" Then Lokasyon = Left(Lokasyon, Len(Lokasyon) - 1)
	MTableT.OKUTULAN_LOKASYON = Lokasyon
	if MTableT.OKUTULAN_LOKASYON = MTableT.LOKASYON and MTableT.SF_MIKTAR = MTableT.OKUTULAN_MIKTAR Then







'	if uBound(LokArray) < 3 Then Exit Sub

'	MTableT.OKUTULAN_LOKASYON = trim(LokArray(0)) & "-" & trim(LokArray(1)) & "-" & Trim(LokArray(2)) & "-" & trim(LokArray(3))
'	if MTableT.OKUTULAN_LOKASYON = MTableT.LOKASYON and MTableT.SF_MIKTAR = MTableT.OKUTULAN_MIKTAR Then
		MTableT.OKUNDU10 = "1"
	Else
		MTableT.OKUNDU10 = "0"
	End if
End Sub
Sub EkraniTemizle()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	
	MTable.Empty
	STOK01_PDA.Empty
	MTableT.Empty
End Sub
Sub SonIslemIptal()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")

	if trim(MTable.STOK60_EVRAKNO) = "" Then Exit Sub

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK60", "")
	Doc1.Load_Voucher MTable.STOK60_EVRAKNO
	Doc1.Delete_Vouhcer
End Sub
Sub DepolarArasiYolIrsaliyesiOlustur()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA_LIST")

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK61", "")
	Set STOK60E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK60E")
	Set STOK61T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK61T")

	ret = Doc1.Load_Voucher(MTable.IRSALIYE_NO)
	if ret <> 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Ýrsaliye yüklenemedi", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	STOK60E.CH_KODU = MTable.CH_KODU
	STOK60E.AMBCODE = MTable.AMBCODE
	STOK60E.TARGETAMBCODE = MTable.TARGETAMBCODE

	For i = 1 to MTableT.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = STOK61T.AddRow()
		STOK61T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

		STOK61T.KOD = MTableT.KOD(i)
		STOK61T.LOTNUMBER = MTableT.LOTNUMBER(i)
		STOK61T.SERINO = MTableT.SERINO(i)
		STOK61T.SF_MIKTAR = MTableT.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		STOK61T.SF_SF_UNIT = MTableT.SF_UNIT(i)
		STOK61T.AMBCODE = MTableT.AMBCODE(i)
		STOK61T.LOCATION1 = MTableT.LOCATION1(i)
		STOK61T.LOCATION2 = MTableT.LOCATION2(i)
		STOK61T.LOCATION3 = MTableT.LOCATION3(i)
		STOK61T.LOCATION4 = MTableT.LOCATION4(i)
		STOK61T.FOKOD = MTableT.FOKOD(i)
		STOK61T.SF_VRI_VR_R1 = MTableT.VRI_VR_R1(i)
		STOK61T.SF_VRI_VR_R2 = MTableT.VRI_VR_R2(i)
		STOK61T.SF_VRI_VR_R3 = MTableT.VRI_VR_R3(i)
		STOK61T.SF_VRI_NUM1 = MTableT.VRI_NUM1(i)
		STOK61T.SF_VRI_NUM2 = MTableT.VRI_NUM2(i)
		STOK61T.SF_VRI_VR_C = MTableT.VRI_VR_C(i)
		STOK61T.OALLOC_SYSSTATUS = MTableT.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(i)
		STOK61T.OALLOC_NONMOVABL = MTableT.ALLOC_NONMOVABL(i)
		STOK61T.OALLOC_EVRAKTYPE = MTableT.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(i)
		STOK61T.OALLOC_EVRAKNO = MTableT.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(i)
		STOK61T.OALLOC_SIPTYPE = MTableT.ALLOC_SIPTYPE(i)
		STOK61T.OALLOC_SIPNO = MTableT.ALLOC_SIPNO(i)
		STOK61T.OALLOC_SIPARTNO = MTableT.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i)
		STOK61T.OALLOC_MPSEVNO = MTableT.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(i)
		STOK61T.OALLOC_MPSARTNO = MTableT.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(i)

		STOK61T.NALLOC_SYSSTATUS = MTableT.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(i)
		STOK61T.NALLOC_NONMOVABL = MTableT.ALLOC_NONMOVABL(i)
		STOK61T.NALLOC_EVRAKTYPE = MTableT.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(i)
		STOK61T.NALLOC_EVRAKNO = MTableT.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(i)
		STOK61T.NALLOC_SIPTYPE = MTableT.ALLOC_SIPTYPE(i)
		STOK61T.NALLOC_SIPNO = MTableT.ALLOC_SIPNO(i)
		STOK61T.NALLOC_SIPARTNO = MTableT.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i)
		STOK61T.NALLOC_MPSEVNO = MTableT.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(i)
		STOK61T.NALLOC_MPSARTNO = MTableT.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(i)
	Next
	ret = Doc1.Save_Voucher
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher

	if ret = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Ýþlem tamamlandý", "", MB_OK
		MTable.Empty
		MTableT.Empty
		Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL
'		Call VarsayilanDepolar()
	End if
End Sub
Sub VarsayilanDepolar()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	MTable.AMBCODE = "HK"
End Sub
Function TumOkutmalarTamamlanmamis()
	TumOkutmalarTamamlanmamis = False

	Set S01L = Doc.Select2("sdf34grhdh", "STOK01_PDA_LIST", "OKUNDU10<>'1'", "", -1)
	if S01L.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Tüm satýrlar okutulmamýþ", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		TumOkutmalarTamamlanmamis = True
	End if
End Function

Baþlangýç Ekran Formu Bu Uygulama için : _GNPRG3
Bu Uygulama için Baþlangýç Ekran Formu : TRACK33_2X1
Baþlangýçta Bu Makro Çalýþacak : Makro1
Alt Ekranlar Ýçin Ekran Formu
Alt Ekran Form Kodu : 
Yeni Alt Ekra Form Kodu: 
Alt Ekran Açýlmadan Önce Çalýþtýrýlacak Makro :
